<tr><td>↳近 tightness</td><td>/ˈtaɪtnəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>吝啬；紧绷</td><td>tightness with expenses<br>在开支上的抠紧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her tightness with expenses annoyed the whole department.  她在开支上的抠紧令整个部门不快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 affluence</td><td>/ˈæfluəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>富裕，丰富</td><td>live in affluence<br>生活富裕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family had lived in quiet affluence for three generations.  这家人已在平静的富裕中生活了三代。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 opulence</td><td>/ˈɑːpjələns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>富裕，丰裕</td><td>live in sheer opulence<br>生活极度奢华</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The palace displayed an opulence that stunned every visiting guest.  这座宫殿展现出令每位来客震惊的奢华。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 richness</td><td>/ˈrɪtʃnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>富有，丰富</td><td>the richness of the collection<br>收藏的丰富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Visitors admired the richness of the museum's holdings.  参观者赞叹博物馆藏品的丰富。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wealth</td><td>/ˈwelθ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量，丰富；财富</td><td>a wealth of experience<br>丰富的经验</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old editor brought a wealth of experience to the team.  这位老编辑给团队带来了丰富的经验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 generosity</td><td>/ˌdʒenəˈrɑːsəti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>慷慨，大方；宽厚</td><td>act of great generosity<br>极为慷慨的举动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library was built through the generosity of one donor.  这座图书馆靠一位捐赠者的慷慨建成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 largesse</td><td>/lɑːrˈdʒes/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>慷慨</td><td>royal largesse<br>王室的慷慨赏赐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum was built entirely through the largesse of one wealthy family.  这座博物馆完全是靠一个富有家族的慷慨捐赠建成的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 munificence</td><td>/mjuːˈnɪfɪsəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>慷慨，大方</td><td>the donor's munificence<br>捐赠者的慷慨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library exists thanks to one man's munificence.  这座图书馆的存在全靠一个人的慷慨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 openhandedness</del></td><td>/ˌoʊpənˈhændɪdnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>慷慨大方</td><td>legendary openhandedness<br>传奇般的慷慨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His openhandedness toward struggling students was legendary in the department.  他对困难学生的慷慨在系里是出了名的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 philanthropy</td><td>/fɪˈlænθrəpi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>慈善，博爱</td><td>corporate philanthropy<br>企业慈善事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She turned to philanthropy after selling the company.  卖掉公司后她转向慈善事业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>perambulate</b></del></td><td>/pəˈræmbjuleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>徒步穿越，走过</td><td>perambulate the old town<br>徒步走遍老城</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every evening the old couple perambulate the narrow streets of the town.  每天傍晚，那对老夫妻都在镇上的窄街间徒步走一圈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amble</td><td>/ˈæmbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>漫步，闲逛</td><td>amble along the beach<br>沿海滩漫步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We ambled along the river until the sun went down.  我们沿着河边漫步，直到日落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cross</td><td>/ˈkrɔːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>反驳，阻挠；穿越</td><td>cross his plans<br>阻挠他的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody dared cross the old chairman in public.  没人敢在公开场合反驳这位老董事长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 go</td><td>/ˈɡoʊ/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>走，行走；去</td><td>go on foot<br>步行前往</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They had to go on foot across the whole valley.  他们不得不步行穿越整个山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 navigate</td><td>/ˈnævəˌɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>航行；穿行，导航</td><td>navigate the winding river<br>沿蜿蜒的河流行进</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She navigated the crowded market with surprising ease.  她轻松自如地穿行于拥挤的市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pass</td><td>/ˈpæs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>通过（法案）；经过</td><td>pass a controversial bill<br>通过一项有争议的法案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senate passed the budget after two nights of debate.  经过两晚辩论，参议院通过了预算案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 peregrinate</del></td><td>/ˈpɛrəɡrəneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>徒步旅行，漫游</td><td>peregrinate through Europe<br>徒步游历欧洲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Each summer he would peregrinate through the villages of northern Spain.  每年夏天他都会漫游西班牙北部的村庄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proceed</td><td>/prəˈsiːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>前进；继续进行</td><td>proceed along the path<br>沿小路前行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We proceeded slowly along the muddy path toward the old bridge.  我们沿着泥泞的小路缓慢地朝老桥走去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ramble</td><td>/ˈræmbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>漫谈，东拉西扯；漫步</td><td>ramble on about<br>喋喋不休地扯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rambled on about his childhood for a full hour.  他絮絮叨叨讲了整整一小时童年往事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transit</td><td>/ˈtrænzɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>通行，通过；运输</td><td>in transit through the city<br>过境穿城而行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The goods were damaged while in transit through the mountains.  货物在穿越山区运输途中受损。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 travel</td><td>/ˈtrævəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>旅行，行走</td><td>travel on foot<br>步行旅行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They traveled on foot across the valley for two long days.  他们徒步穿越山谷走了漫长的两天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 traverse</td><td>/trəˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反驳，否认；横穿</td><td>traverse the allegation<br>反驳该指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defense traversed every allegation made by the prosecution.  辩方否认了检方提出的每一项指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>perceptible</b></td><td>/pərˈseptəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可察觉的</td><td>a barely perceptible change<br>几乎察觉不到的变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a barely perceptible change in her tone of voice.  她的语调有一丝几乎察觉不到的变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appreciable</td><td>/əˈpriːʃəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可感知的，明显的</td><td>an appreciable difference<br>明显的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is no appreciable difference between these two brands of paint.  这两个牌子的油漆之间没有明显的差别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 apprehensible</del></td><td>/ˌæprɪˈhensəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的</td><td>readily apprehensible ideas<br>易于理解的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument is barely apprehensible to readers without training.  他的论证对没有受过训练的读者几乎难以理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detectable</td><td>/dɪˈtektəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可检测到的，可察觉的</td><td>detectable levels of lead<br>可检测到的铅含量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new sensor finds detectable traces of gas within seconds.  新传感器几秒内就能发现可检测到的气体痕迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discernible</td><td>/dɪˈsɜːrnəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可辨别的，可看出的</td><td>no discernible pattern<br>看不出规律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was no discernible improvement after three weeks of treatment.  治疗三周后没有可察觉的改善。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinguishable</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可区分的，可辨别的</td><td>clearly distinguishable from others<br>与其他明显可区分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The twins are barely distinguishable except for a small scar.  这对双胞胎除了一道小疤几乎无法区分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 palpable</td><td>/ˈpælpəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，易觉察的</td><td>a palpable sense of relief<br>明显的宽慰感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A palpable tension filled the room as the verdict was read aloud.  宣读判决时，房间里弥漫着明显的紧张气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sensible</td><td>/ˈsensəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明智的，合理的</td><td>a sensible choice<br>明智的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing boots was a sensible choice in that mud.  在那种泥地里穿靴子是明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impalpable</td><td>/ɪmˈpælpəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以察觉的，无形的</td><td>an impalpable difference<br>难以察觉的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A faint, impalpable tension hung over the negotiating table.  谈判桌上笼罩着一种难以察觉的紧张气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 imperceptible</td><td>/ˌɪmpərˈseptɪbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>察觉不到的，极微小的</td><td>an imperceptible shift<br>察觉不到的变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The change in temperature was almost imperceptible to the guests.  客人几乎察觉不到温度的变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 inappreciable</del></td><td>/ˌɪnəˈpriʃəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的，感觉不到的</td><td>an inappreciable amount<br>微不足道的量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The risk from such a tiny dose is inappreciable.  如此微小剂量带来的风险微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indistinguishable</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法区分的，难以辨别的</td><td>indistinguishable from the original<br>与原件无法区分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The copy was almost indistinguishable from the original painting.  这份复制品与原画几乎无法区分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insensible</td><td>/ɪnˈsensəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知觉的，昏迷的</td><td>insensible to pain<br>感觉不到疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blow left him insensible for several minutes.  这一击使他几分钟内失去了知觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undetectable</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈtektəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法检测到的，察觉不到的</td><td>undetectable by radar<br>雷达无法探测到</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus remained undetectable in blood tests for several weeks.  这种病毒在血液检测中数周都无法检出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>peremptory</b></td><td>/pəˈremptəri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不容置疑的；专横傲慢的</td><td>a peremptory order<br>不容置疑的命令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His peremptory tone left no room for questions or discussion.  他傲慢的语气不给任何提问或讨论的余地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compulsory</td><td>/kəmˈpʌlsəri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强制的，必修的</td><td>compulsory education<br>义务教育</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing a helmet is compulsory on every construction site.  在每个建筑工地戴安全帽都是强制的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forced</td><td>/ˈfɔːrst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>被迫的，强迫的</td><td>a forced confession<br>被迫的供词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court threw out the forced confession as unreliable.  法庭以不可靠为由驳回了这份被迫的供词。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imperative</td><td>/ɪmˈperətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>命令的，强制性的；迫切的</td><td>an imperative need<br>迫切的需要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is imperative that we repair the dam before the rains arrive.  在雨季来临前修好大坝是迫切必需的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incumbent</td><td>/ɪnˈkʌmbənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>义不容辞的，必须的</td><td>incumbent on the manager<br>经理义不容辞的事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is incumbent on every driver to check the brakes regularly.  定期检查刹车是每位司机义不容辞的责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 involuntary</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlənteri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非自愿的；无意识的</td><td>an involuntary reaction<br>不自觉的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Blinking is an involuntary response to sudden light.  眨眼是对突然强光的不自觉反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obligatory</td><td>/əˈblɪɡətɔːri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强制性的</td><td>obligatory military service<br>强制的兵役</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In that country obligatory military service lasts for eighteen months.  在那个国家，强制的兵役要服十八个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 required</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>必需的，规定的</td><td>required reading<br>必读材料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A signed form is required before the trip can begin.  出行前必须提交一份签好的表格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 assumptive</del></td><td>/əˈsʌmptɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，自以为是的；假定的</td><td>an assumptive attitude<br>傲慢的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His assumptive manner annoyed everyone at the negotiating table.  他傲慢的态度让谈判桌上的人都很反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bumptious</td><td>/ˈbʌmpʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>专横傲慢的，自以为是的</td><td>a bumptious young man<br>自以为是的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her bumptious manner annoyed the older members of the committee.  她自以为是的态度让委员会里年长的成员很反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 haughty</td><td>/ˈhɔːti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，目中无人的</td><td>a haughty manner<br>傲慢的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave the waiter a haughty look and said nothing.  她傲慢地看了服务员一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imperious</td><td>/ɪmˈpɪriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，专横的</td><td>an imperious tone<br>专横的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her imperious manner made junior colleagues afraid to disagree.  她专横的态度让年轻同事不敢有异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lofty</td><td>/ˈlɔːfti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>崇高的；自大的</td><td>a lofty tone<br>自大的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dismissed our idea with a lofty wave of her hand.  她自大地一挥手就否定了我们的想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pompous</td><td>/ˈpɑːmpəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>浮夸的；傲慢的</td><td>a pompous speech<br>浮夸的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pompous speech bored everyone in the crowded town hall.  那场浮夸的演讲让拥挤的市政厅里每个人都厌烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presumptuous</td><td>/prɪˈzʌmptʃuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冒失的，放肆的</td><td>presumptuous of you<br>你太放肆了</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seems presumptuous to criticize a book you have not read.  批评一本没读过的书，似乎太放肆了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pretentious</td><td>/prɪˈtenʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>浮夸的，自命不凡的</td><td>pretentious prose<br>浮夸的文风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His pretentious speech annoyed almost everyone in the audience.  他浮夸的演讲让几乎所有听众反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supercilious</td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈsɪliəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>高傲的，傲慢的</td><td>a supercilious smile<br>傲慢的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A supercilious smile crossed his face when he heard my question.  听到我的问题，他脸上掠过傲慢的微笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 elective</td><td>/ɪˈlektɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>可自由选择的；选举的</td><td>an elective course<br>选修课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of the surgery performed here is elective, not urgent.  这里做的多数手术是可选择的，而非紧急的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 optional</td><td>/ˈɑːpʃənəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可选择的，非强制的</td><td>an optional extra course<br>一门选修的额外课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Attendance at the evening seminar is entirely optional.  参加晚间研讨会完全是自愿的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 voluntary</td><td>/ˈvɑːlənteri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自愿的，志愿的</td><td>voluntary participation<br>自愿参与</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Attendance at the evening seminar is entirely voluntary.  晚间研讨会完全自愿参加。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humble</td><td>/ˈhʌmbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑微的，地位低下的；谦逊的</td><td>humble origins<br>卑微的出身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rose from humble origins to lead the company.  他出身卑微，最终领导了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lowly</td><td>/ˈloʊli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>地位低的，卑微的</td><td>a lowly clerk<br>一个卑微的职员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He began as a lowly assistant and rose to direct the firm.  他从卑微的助理做起，后来掌管了公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 modest</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谦虚的；不大的，适度的</td><td>modest about her success<br>对成功很谦虚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed modest about his award and thanked his team.  他对自己的奖项很谦虚，还感谢了团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>parochial</b></td><td>/pəˈroʊkiəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狭隘的</td><td>a parochial outlook<br>狭隘的眼界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His parochial outlook kept him from appreciating other cultures.  他狭隘的眼界使他无法欣赏其他文化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insular</td><td>/ˈɪnsələr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（观念、想法等）孤立狭隘的</td><td>an insular attitude<br>狭隘的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His insular attitude toward foreign customs cost him many friendships abroad.  他对外国习俗的狭隘态度使他在国外失去了许多友谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 narrow-minded</td><td>/ˌnæroʊ ˈmaɪndɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>心胸狭窄的，偏狭的</td><td>narrow-minded prejudice<br>狭隘的偏见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His narrow-minded remarks embarrassed everyone at the table.  他狭隘的言论让全桌人尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provincial</td><td>/prəˈvɪnʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>狭隘（的）</td><td>provincial tastes<br>狭隘的品味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His provincial tastes made him suspicious of anything foreign or unfamiliar.  他狭隘的品味使他对一切外来或陌生的事物都怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sectarian</td><td>/sekˈteriən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狭隘的</td><td>sectarian prejudice<br>狭隘的派系偏见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His sectarian views blinded him to the merits of other traditions.  他狭隘的派系观念使他看不到其他传统的优点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 broad-minded</td><td>/ˌbrɔd ˈmaɪndəd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想开明的，宽容的</td><td>a broad-minded editor<br>开明的编辑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her broad-minded parents let her study art abroad.  她开明的父母让她出国学艺术。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 catholic</td><td>/ˈkæθlɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>普遍的，包容的</td><td>catholic tastes<br>广泛的爱好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her catholic tastes in music surprised everyone at the party.  她在音乐上广泛的爱好让聚会上每个人都惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cosmopolitan</td><td>/ˌkɑːzməˈpɑːlɪtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有世界性眼光的，包容的</td><td>a cosmopolitan city<br>国际化的都市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years abroad gave her a cosmopolitan outlook her old classmates clearly lacked.  多年国外生活给了她老同学明显缺乏的世界性眼光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liberal</td><td>/ˈlɪbərəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想前卫的；慷慨的，大方的</td><td>liberal social views<br>前卫的社会观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His liberal social views often surprised the older members of the club.  他前卫的社会观念常常让俱乐部里年长的成员感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 open-minded</td><td>/ˈoʊpən ˈmaɪndɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想开明的</td><td>an open-minded editor<br>思想开明的编辑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good reviewer stays open-minded about unfamiliar musical styles.  好的评论者对陌生的音乐风格保持开明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>perfidy</b></td><td>/ˈpɜːrfədi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不忠，背信弃义</td><td>an act of perfidy<br>背信弃义的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The perfidy of the general cost the alliance both territory and trust.  那位将军的背信弃义使联盟失去了领土和信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 backstabbing</td><td>/ˈbækstæbɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>背后中伤，暗中伤害</td><td>constant office backstabbing<br>办公室里不断的暗算</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant backstabbing among colleagues poisoned the atmosphere in the small office.  同事间不断的暗中伤害毒化了这间小办公室的气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disloyalty</td><td>/dɪsˈlɔɪəlti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不忠，背信</td><td>accused of disloyalty<br>被指不忠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of disloyalty to the party leadership.  他被指对党的领导层不忠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infidelity</td><td>/ˌɪnfɪˈdelɪti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>不忠，背信</td><td>marital infidelity<br>婚内不忠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The couple separated after years of quarrels and infidelity.  这对夫妻在多年争吵和背叛后分居了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sellout</td><td>/ˈseˌlaʊt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>背叛；出卖原则者</td><td>be branded a sellout<br>被指为叛徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Former allies called him a sellout after he joined the opposition.  在他投靠反对派后，昔日的盟友称他为叛徒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 treachery</td><td>/ˈtretʃəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>背叛，变节</td><td>an act of treachery<br>一次背叛行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was executed for treachery after selling secrets to the enemy.  他因向敌人出卖机密犯下叛国罪而被处死。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unfaithfulness</td><td>/ʌnˈfeɪθfəlnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不忠，背信</td><td>unfaithfulness to a friend<br>对朋友的背弃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unfaithfulness to the cause shocked everyone who had trusted her.  她对事业的背弃让所有信任过她的人震惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 double cross</td><td>/ˈdʌbəl krɔs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>背叛，出卖</td><td>a cruel double cross<br>残忍的背叛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smuggler's double cross left his partners in prison.  这个走私犯的背叛让他的同伙进了监狱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 allegiance</td><td>/əˈliːdʒəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>忠诚，效忠</td><td>swear allegiance to<br>宣誓效忠于</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New citizens pledge allegiance to the country at the ceremony.  新公民在仪式上宣誓效忠国家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 devotion</td><td>/dɪˈvoʊʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>忠诚，挚爱；献身</td><td>devotion to duty<br>忠于职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her devotion to her students earned her wide respect.  她对学生的挚爱赢得了广泛的尊敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 faithfulness</td><td>/ˈfeɪθfəlnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>忠诚，忠实</td><td>faithfulness to a promise<br>信守承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was rewarded for years of faithfulness to the family business.  他因多年忠于家族企业而获得回报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fidelity</td><td>/fɪˈdeləti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>忠诚</td><td>fidelity to his promise<br>对诺言的忠诚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fidelity to the promise surprised everyone who knew him.  他对诺言的忠诚让所有认识他的人都感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 loyalty</td><td>/ˈlɔɪəlti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>忠诚，忠实</td><td>loyalty to the team<br>对团队的忠诚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of loyalty finally earned him the manager's trust.  多年的忠诚终于赢得了经理的信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 staunchness</del></td><td>/ˈstɔntʃnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>忠贞，坚定不移</td><td>staunchness of loyalty<br>忠诚的坚定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His staunchness in defending his friend impressed even his rivals.  他坚定不移地为朋友辩护，甚至令对手佩服。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 steadfastness</td><td>/ˈstedˌfæstnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>坚定不移，忠贞</td><td>steadfastness in adversity<br>逆境中的坚定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her steadfastness during the strike inspired the other workers.  她在罢工期间的坚定不移鼓舞了其他工人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>perforate</b></td><td>/ˈpɜːrfəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>打孔，穿透</td><td>perforate the metal sheet<br>在金属板上打孔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The machine perforates each sheet so the pages tear off neatly.  这台机器在每张纸上打孔，使页面能整齐撕下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bore</td><td>/bɔːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>令人厌烦的人或事物</td><td>an unbearable old bore<br>让人受不了的老唠叨鬼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My uncle is such a bore that guests leave early.  我叔叔特别无趣，客人们都早早离席。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drill</td><td>/ˈdrɪl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>钻孔，钻透</td><td>drill a small hole<br>钻一个小孔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers drilled a small hole through the concrete wall.  工人们在混凝土墙上钻了一个小孔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hole</td><td>/ˈhoʊl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>困境，窘境；洞</td><td>get into a hole<br>陷入窘境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bad loans put the small company in a very deep hole.  不良贷款让这家小公司陷入很深的困境。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pierce</td><td>/ˈpɪrs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>刺穿，穿透</td><td>pierce the thick leather<br>刺穿厚皮革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The needle pierced the thick leather without much effort.  针没费多大力气就刺穿了厚皮革。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 punch</td><td>/ˈpʌntʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>用拳猛击</td><td>punch a hole in it<br>在上面打个洞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He punched the wall and broke two knuckles.  他猛击墙壁，弄断了两根指骨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 puncture</td><td>/ˈpʌŋktʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>刺破，戳孔</td><td>puncture a tire<br>把轮胎刺破</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A rusty nail punctured the front tire of my bicycle.  一根锈钉刺破了我自行车的前胎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 riddle</td><td>/ˈrɪdəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>谜语，难题</td><td>answer a riddle<br>解答谜语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man asked a riddle that nobody in the village could answer.  老人问了一个村里没人能答的谜语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>perfunctory</b></td><td>/pərˈfʌŋktəri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>敷衍的，草率的</td><td>a perfunctory nod<br>敷衍的点头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave the report a perfunctory glance and signed it immediately.  他敷衍地扫了一眼报告就立刻签了字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 automatic</td><td>/ˌɔːtəˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>机械的，不经思索的；自动的</td><td>an automatic apology<br>机械的道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He murmured an automatic apology and hurried past me.  他机械地含糊道了个歉，匆匆从我身边走过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cursory</td><td>/ˈkɜːrsəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，不注意细节的</td><td>a cursory glance<br>匆匆一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cursory inspection missed the cracks running along the bridge supports.  一次匆忙的检查漏掉了桥墩上的裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mechanical</td><td>/məˈkænɪkəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>机械的；敷衍呆板的</td><td>a mechanical response<br>机械敷衍的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a mechanical nod without listening to a single word.  他机械地点了点头，一个字也没听。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 superficial</td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈfɪʃl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表面的，肤浅的</td><td>a superficial understanding<br>肤浅的理解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has only a superficial knowledge of the subject.  她对这门学科只有肤浅的了解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>perimeter</b></td><td>/pəˈrɪmɪtər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>边界，界限</td><td>guard the perimeter<br>守卫外围边界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soldiers guarded the perimeter of the camp throughout the night.  士兵们整夜守卫着营地的周边界线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 borderline</td><td>/ˈbɔːrdərlaɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>边界，界线；界线上的</td><td>on the borderline<br>处在界线上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village sits right on the borderline between two provinces.  这个村子正好位于两省的边界上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bound</td><td>/baʊnd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>界限；决心的，一定会的</td><td>within the bounds of law<br>在法律范围内</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her curiosity knew no bounds when she entered the old library.  她走进那座旧图书馆时，好奇心毫无边界。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 boundary</td><td>/ˈbaʊndəri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>边界，界限</td><td>the boundary between fields<br>田地之间的边界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A low stone wall marks the boundary between the farms.  一道矮石墙标出两个农场的边界。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 circumference</td><td>/sərˈkʌmfərəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>圆周，周长</td><td>the circumference of the circle<br>圆的周长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Measure the circumference of the trunk at chest height.  在胸高处测量树干的周长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confines</td><td>/ˈkɑːnˌfaɪnz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>范围，界限</td><td>the confines of the campus<br>校园的范围</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students must stay within the confines of the campus.  学生必须待在校园范围内。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 edge</td><td>/ˈedʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>边缘，边沿</td><td>the edge of the cliff<br>悬崖边缘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stood at the edge of the cliff and looked down.  她站在悬崖边缘往下看。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frame</td><td>/ˈfreɪm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>制定，构想；框架</td><td>frame a new policy<br>制定新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee framed a new policy on remote work overnight.  委员会一夜之间制定了新的远程工作政策。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fringe</td><td>/frɪndʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>边缘</td><td>on the fringe of society<br>处于社会边缘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel describes families living on the fringe of society.  这部小说描写生活在社会边缘的家庭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 margin</td><td>/ˈmɑːrdʒən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>边缘；页边空白；差额</td><td>the margin of the lake<br>湖的边缘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tall reeds grow along the margin of the lake.  高高的芦苇沿着湖边生长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 periphery</td><td>/pəˈrɪfəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>外围，边缘</td><td>the periphery of the city<br>城市的外围</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New factories sprang up on the periphery of the city.  新工厂在城市外围拔地而起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skirt</td><td>/skɜːrt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>绕行，避开</td><td>skirt the issue<br>回避问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister tried to skirt the issue with a long, vague answer.  部长试图用冗长含糊的回答回避这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 verge</td><td>/ˈvɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>边缘，边界</td><td>on the verge of collapse<br>在崩溃的边缘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company was on the verge of collapse last winter.  去年冬天这家公司濒临倒闭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 center</td><td>/ˈsentər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>使集中；中心</td><td>center attention on quality<br>把注意力集中于质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report centers its argument on three very clear examples.  报告把论点集中在三个非常清晰的例子上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 core</td><td>/ˈkɔːr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>核心，要点</td><td>the core of the argument<br>论点的核心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At the core of the dispute lies a simple money question.  争端的核心是一个简单的金钱问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heart</td><td>/ˈhɑːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>核心，中心；心脏</td><td>the heart of the matter<br>问题的核心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Let us get to the heart of the matter quickly.  我们快点谈到问题的核心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 kernel</td><td>/ˈkɜːrnəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>核心，要点；果核</td><td>the kernel of truth<br>一点真实的核心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a kernel of truth in this otherwise absurd rumor.  这个荒唐的谣言里有一点真实的核心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>periodical</b></td><td>/ˌpɪriˈɑːdɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>周期性的，有固定间隔的</td><td>periodical inspections<br>定期检查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline carries out periodical inspections of every engine and wing.  该航空公司对每台发动机和机翼进行定期检查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 continual</td><td>/kənˈtɪnjuəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>连续的，不断的</td><td>continual interruptions<br>不断的打扰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Continual delays finally forced them to cancel the trip.  接连不断的延误最终迫使他们取消行程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 periodic</td><td>/ˌpɪriˈɑːdɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>周期性的，定期的</td><td>periodic safety inspections<br>定期安全检查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge undergoes periodic checks every six months.  这座桥每六个月接受一次定期检查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recurrent</td><td>/rɪˈkɜːrənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反复出现的</td><td>a recurrent theme<br>反复出现的主题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loneliness is a recurrent theme in her early poems.  孤独是她早期诗作中反复出现的主题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recurring</td><td>/rɪˈkɜːrɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>反复发生的</td><td>a recurring dream<br>反复出现的梦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He suffers from a recurring pain in his left knee.  他左膝反复疼痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 constant</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不变的，恒定的；忠诚的</td><td>a constant friend<br>忠诚不变的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her support remained constant through every difficult year.  她的支持在每个艰难年头都不曾改变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 continuous</td><td>/kənˈtɪnjuːəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>连续的，不间断的</td><td>a continuous line<br>一条连续的线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The machine has been in continuous operation for six days.  这台机器已连续运转六天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incessant</td><td>/ɪnˈsesnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无间断的</td><td>incessant noise<br>不间断的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incessant drumming from next door kept us awake all night.  隔壁不间断的鼓声让我们整夜无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unceasing</td><td>/ənˈsiːsɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不停的，持续的</td><td>unceasing effort<br>不懈的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unceasing questions finally uncovered the truth.  她不停的追问最终揭开了真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>peripatetic</b></td><td>/ˌperipəˈtetɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巡游的，四处游历的</td><td>a peripatetic career<br>四处奔波的职业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peripatetic photographer rarely slept in the same city twice.  这位四处游历的摄影师很少在同一座城市住两次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ambulant</td><td>/ˈæmbjələnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>流动的；能走动的</td><td>an ambulant patient<br>能走动的病人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic treats ambulant patients who do not need a bed.  这家诊所治疗无需住院的能走动病人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 errant</td><td>/ˈerənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>误入歧途的，犯错误的</td><td>an errant husband<br>出轨的丈夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The errant student was suspended after ignoring three separate warnings.  这名行为不端的学生在忽视三次警告后被停学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nomadic</td><td>/noʊˈmædɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>游牧的，流浪的</td><td>a nomadic lifestyle<br>游牧的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her nomadic childhood took her through eleven countries.  她流浪的童年带她走过十一个国家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 perambulatory</del></td><td>/pəˈræmbjələtɔri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>漫游的，巡行的</td><td>a perambulatory habit<br>漫游的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old scholar kept his perambulatory habit into old age.  这位老学者到晚年仍保持漫游的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peregrine</td><td>/ˈperəˌɡriːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>漂泊的，来自外地的</td><td>a peregrine lifestyle<br>漂泊不定的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He led a peregrine life, never staying in one city long.  他过着漂泊的生活，从不在一座城市久留。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vagabond</td><td>/ˈvæɡəbɑnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>流浪者；流浪的</td><td>a vagabond life<br>流浪的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He led a vagabond life among the river towns.  他在河边小镇间过着流浪生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wandering</td><td>/ˈwɑːndərɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>游荡的；散漫的</td><td>a wandering discussion<br>散漫的讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wandering argument never reached any clear conclusion.  这番散漫的论述始终没有明确结论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sedentary</td><td>/ˈsednteri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固定不动的</td><td>a sedentary lifestyle<br>久坐不动的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of sedentary office work had weakened his back and shoulders.  多年久坐不动的办公工作削弱了他的背和肩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 settled</td><td>/ˈsetəld/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固定的，安定的</td><td>a settled routine<br>固定的作息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years of moving they finally led a settled life.  多年漂泊后他们终于过上安定的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>peripheral</b></td><td>/pəˈrɪfərəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>辅助性的，次要的</td><td>of peripheral importance<br>只具次要意义</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That issue is peripheral to our main discussion today.  那个问题对我们今天的主要讨论来说是次要的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 accessorial</del></td><td>/ˌæksəˈsɔriəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>附属的，辅助的</td><td>accessorial charges<br>附加费用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The invoice listed several accessorial charges for storage and handling.  发票上列了几项仓储和搬运的附加费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accessory</td><td>/əkˈsesəri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>辅助的，附属的</td><td>an accessory building<br>一座附属建筑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The garage is an accessory structure behind the main house.  车库是主屋后面的一座附属建筑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 appurtenant</del></td><td>/əˈpɜrtənənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>附属的，附加的</td><td>appurtenant rights<br>附属权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The garage and the driveway are appurtenant to the main house.  车库和车道是主屋的附属部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supplemental</td><td>/ˌsʌpləˈmentəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>补充的，附加的</td><td>supplemental income<br>补充性收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took a night job for supplemental income during college.  上大学时她做夜班工作以补充收入。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supplementary</td><td>/ˌsʌpləˈmentəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>补充的，额外的</td><td>supplementary information<br>补充信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher handed out supplementary reading for the weekend.  老师发了周末的补充阅读材料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 chief</td><td>/ˈtʃiːf/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>主要的，首要的；首领</td><td>the chief reason<br>主要原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cost was the chief reason they abandoned the project.  成本是他们放弃这个项目的主要原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 main</td><td>/ˈmeɪn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>主要的，最重要的</td><td>the main point<br>主要论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The main problem is that we have almost no time.  主要问题是我们几乎没有时间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 principal</td><td>/ˈprɪnsəpəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>主要的，首要的；校长</td><td>the principal cause<br>首要原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tourism is the principal source of income on the island.  旅游业是这个岛上的主要收入来源。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>perish</b></td><td>/ˈperɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>死亡，消亡</td><td>perish in the fire<br>在火灾中丧生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dozens of animals perished when the barn caught fire that night.  那晚谷仓失火，数十只动物丧生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decease</td><td>/dɪˈsiːs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>死亡，去世</td><td>since his decease<br>自他去世以来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The estate was divided among the children after her decease.  她去世后遗产在子女间分割。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demise</td><td>/dɪˈmaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>死亡；终结，灭亡</td><td>the demise of the empire<br>帝国的灭亡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians still debate the causes of the empire's sudden demise.  历史学家仍在争论该帝国突然灭亡的原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 die</td><td>/ˈdaɪ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>逐渐减弱，消失；死亡</td><td>die down<br>渐渐平息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The applause slowly died away as the lights dimmed.  灯光变暗，掌声渐渐消失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 end</td><td>/ˈend/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>结束；末端</td><td>end the war<br>结束战争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike ended after both sides agreed on wages.  双方就工资达成一致后罢工结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expire</td><td>/ɪkˈspaɪər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>断气，死亡；到期</td><td>the contract expires<br>合同到期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your passport expires next month, so apply for a new one.  你的护照下个月到期，所以要申请新的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 succumb</td><td>/səˈkʌm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>屈服；死亡</td><td>succumb to pressure<br>屈服于压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor finally succumbed to pressure and withdrew the unpopular proposal.  市长最终屈服于压力，撤回了不受欢迎的提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pass away</td><td>/pæs əˈweɪ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>去世，逝去</td><td>pass away peacefully<br>安详离世</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her grandmother passed away peacefully in her sleep last night.  她的祖母昨夜在睡梦中安详离世。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 breathe</td><td>/ˈbriːð/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>呼吸；活着</td><td>breathe freely again<br>重新自由呼吸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He could hardly breathe after climbing all those steep stairs.  爬完那么多陡台阶后他几乎喘不上气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 live</td><td>/ˈlɪv/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>活着，生存</td><td>live to a great age<br>活到高龄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her great-grandfather lived to a great age in the countryside.  她的曾祖父在乡下活到了很大年纪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 survive</td><td>/sərˈvaɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>存活，幸存</td><td>survive the harsh winter<br>度过严冬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a few seedlings survived the unusually harsh winter.  只有少数几棵幼苗在异常严酷的冬天存活下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>permanent</b></td><td>/ˈpɜːrmənənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>a permanent solution<br>永久的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They are seeking a permanent solution rather than another quick fix.  他们在寻找永久的解决办法，而不是又一个权宜之计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ceaseless</td><td>/ˈsiːslɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不停的，持续的</td><td>ceaseless noise<br>不停的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceaseless traffic outside made it impossible for us to sleep.  外面不停的车流让我们无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dateless</td><td>/ˈdeɪtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>古老得无从考证的</td><td>dateless folk songs<br>年代不明的民歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These dateless legends were told long before anyone wrote them down.  这些年代不明的传说早在有人记录之前就流传着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deathless</td><td>/ˈdɛθləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不死的，不朽的</td><td>deathless fame<br>不朽的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet won deathless fame with a single slender volume.  这位诗人凭一本薄薄的诗集赢得了不朽名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endless</td><td>/ˈendləs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无尽的，无休止的</td><td>endless complaints<br>无休止的抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The meeting felt endless, though it lasted only ninety minutes.  会议感觉没完没了，其实只开了九十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eternal</td><td>/ɪˈtɜːrnl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>eternal life<br>永生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poem promises the beloved eternal youth.  这首诗许诺爱人永恒的青春。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immortal</td><td>/ɪˈmɔːrtəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不朽的，永生的</td><td>immortal fame<br>不朽的声名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet's immortal lines are still quoted two thousand years later.  这位诗人不朽的诗句两千年后仍被引用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perpetual</td><td>/pərˈpetʃuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不断的</td><td>perpetual motion<br>永恒运动，永动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lives in perpetual fear of losing his job.  他一直生活在失业的恐惧中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undying</td><td>/ʌnˈdaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不灭的</td><td>undying love<br>永恒的爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She pledged her undying loyalty to the cause she had joined.  她宣誓对所投身的事业永葆忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unending</td><td>/ʌnˈendɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无尽的，不停的</td><td>an unending stream of visitors<br>络绎不绝的来客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An unending stream of visitors filled the museum all summer.  整个夏天络绎不绝的游客挤满了博物馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ephemeral</td><td>/ɪˈfemərəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，转瞬即逝的</td><td>ephemeral fame<br>短暂的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Internet fame is often ephemeral and quickly forgotten.  网络名声往往短暂，很快被遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 evanescent</td><td>/ˌevəˈnesnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，逐渐消失的</td><td>evanescent beauty<br>转瞬即逝的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The evanescent glow of fireflies lit the summer path.  萤火虫短暂的微光照亮了夏夜小径。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 transient</td><td>/ˈtrænʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的；暂时的；过往的</td><td>transient population<br>流动人口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fame in the internet age is often transient and quickly forgotten.  网络时代的名声往往短暂，很快被人遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 transitory</td><td>/ˈtrænsətɔːri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的</td><td>transitory pleasure<br>短暂的快乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His anger was transitory and he apologized within the hour.  他的愤怒很短暂，一小时内就道了歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>permeable</b></td><td>/ˈpɜːrmiəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可渗透的</td><td>a permeable rock layer<br>可渗水的岩层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water collects here because the rock layer below is highly permeable.  水在这里积聚，因为下面的岩层极易渗水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 passable</td><td>/ˈpæsəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可通行的；尚可的</td><td>a passable mountain road<br>可通行的山路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The road is barely passable after the spring floods.  春季洪水过后，这条路勉强可以通行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 penetrable</td><td>/ˈpenɪtrəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可穿透的，可渗透的</td><td>easily penetrable armor<br>易被穿透的护甲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thin plywood is penetrable by an ordinary nail.  普通钉子就能穿透薄胶合板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pervious</td><td>/ˈpɜːrviəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可渗透的</td><td>pervious concrete surface<br>透水的混凝土路面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers laid a pervious pavement to reduce street flooding.  工程师铺设了透水路面以减少街道积水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 porous</td><td>/ˈpɔːrəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多孔可渗透的</td><td>porous volcanic rock<br>多孔的火山岩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water seeps very quickly through the porous volcanic rock here.  水很快渗过这里多孔的火山岩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 waterproof</td><td>/ˈwɔːtərpruːf/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>防水的；使防水</td><td>waterproof fabric<br>防水面料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This jacket is made of waterproof fabric and dries quickly.  这件夹克用防水面料制成，干得很快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pernicious</b></td><td>/pərˈnɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的</td><td>a pernicious rumor<br>极有害的谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A pernicious rumor about the school spread quickly through the town.  一则关于学校的极有害谣言迅速在镇上传开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adverse</td><td>/ædˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，不利的</td><td>adverse side effects<br>不良副作用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The drug had adverse effects on several elderly patients.  这种药对几位老年病人产生了不良影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baleful</td><td>/ˈbeɪlfl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的</td><td>a baleful influence<br>有害的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His baleful influence on the young players worried the coach.  他对年轻球员的有害影响让教练担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 damaging</td><td>/ˈdæmɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，破坏性的</td><td>damaging to reputation<br>有损声誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The leaked memo proved damaging to the minister's career.  泄露的备忘录对部长的仕途造成损害。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deleterious</td><td>/ˌdeləˈtɪriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的</td><td>deleterious effects on health<br>对健康的有害影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long night shifts had a deleterious effect on the nurses' health.  长期夜班对护士们的健康产生了有害影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detrimental</td><td>/ˌdetrəˈmentəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，不利的</td><td>detrimental to your health<br>对你的健康有害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too much screen time is detrimental to a child's eyesight.  过多看屏幕对孩子的视力有害。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hurtful</td><td>/ˈhɜːrtfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>伤人的，有害的</td><td>hurtful remarks<br>伤人的言语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His hurtful remarks stayed with her for years afterward.  他伤人的话此后多年一直留在她心里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 injurious</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒʊriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，造成伤害的</td><td>injurious to health<br>对健康有害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long hours of night work can be injurious to your health.  长时间夜班工作可能有害健康。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mischievous</td><td>/ˈmɪstʃɪvəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的；调皮的</td><td>a mischievous rumor<br>有害的谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mischievous rumor damaged her reputation for several years.  这个有害的谣言损害了她多年的名誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nocuous</del></td><td>/ˈnɑːkjuəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，有毒的</td><td>nocuous fumes<br>有害的烟气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Factory chimneys released nocuous gases into the evening air.  工厂烟囱把有害气体排入夜晚的空气中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noxious</td><td>/ˈnɑːkʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，有毒的；产生强烈厌恶的</td><td>noxious fumes<br>有毒的烟气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Noxious fumes from the factory forced the school to close for two days.  工厂排出的有毒烟气迫使学校停课两天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 anodyne</td><td>/ˈænədaɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的</td><td>anodyne remarks<br>不痛不痒的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The spokesman gave anodyne answers that offended nobody at all.  发言人给出的回答不痛不痒，谁也没得罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 benign</td><td>/bɪˈnaɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的</td><td>a benign influence<br>有益无害的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The change in policy proved benign for most small farmers.  事实证明，政策变化对大多数小农户是无害的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harmless</td><td>/ˈhɑːrmləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的，不会造成伤害的</td><td>a harmless little joke<br>无伤大雅的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The snake looks frightening but is completely harmless to humans.  这条蛇看起来可怕，但对人完全无害。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 innocent</td><td>/ˈɪnəsənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的，无恶意的；无辜的</td><td>an innocent remark<br>无恶意的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What began as an innocent joke ended in a serious quarrel.  一句本无恶意的玩笑最后引发了激烈争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 innocuous</td><td>/ɪˈnɑːkjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的；不会引起敌意的</td><td>an innocuous question<br>一个无恶意的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What seemed an innocuous question turned out to reveal her deepest secret.  看似无恶意的问题最终却揭露了她最深的秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inoffensive</td><td>/ˌɪnəˈfensɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的，不讨人厌的</td><td>inoffensive background music<br>不讨人厌的背景音乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film is bland but inoffensive enough for family viewing.  这部电影平淡，但足够温和，适合全家观看。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 safe</td><td>/ˈseɪf/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>安全的，可靠的</td><td>a safe choice<br>稳妥的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Taking the train is the safe choice in this weather.  这种天气坐火车是稳妥的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>patchwork</b></td><td>/ˈpætʃwɜːrk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>拼凑物；拼凑而成的</td><td>a patchwork of fields<br>拼块般的田野</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the plane the valley looked like a patchwork of green fields.  从飞机上看，山谷像是一块块绿色田地拼成的图案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assortment</td><td>/əˈsɔːrtmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>各式各样的一批</td><td>an assortment of tools<br>各式工具</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The box held an odd assortment of buttons and coins.  盒子里装着一堆杂七杂八的纽扣和硬币。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hodgepodge</td><td>/ˈhɑːdʒpɑːdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩</td><td>a hodgepodge of styles<br>风格的大杂烩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The house is a hodgepodge of every style he admired.  这房子是他欣赏过的各种风格的大杂烩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 medley</td><td>/ˈmedli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩，混合物</td><td>a medley of flavors<br>各种风味的混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The buffet table offered a medley of flavors from six different countries.  自助餐台上供应着来自六个不同国家的各式风味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 montage</td><td>/mɑːnˈtɑːʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩</td><td>a montage of images<br>图像的拼贴组合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exhibit opened with a montage of images from a century of protest.  展览以一组来自百年抗争的图像拼贴开场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 motley</td><td>/ˈmɑːtli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混杂的，富于变化的</td><td>a motley crowd<br>混杂的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A motley crowd of farmers, students, and shopkeepers filled the town square.  农民、学生和店主组成的混杂人群挤满了市镇广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pastiche</td><td>/pæˈstiːʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>（带嘲讽的）模仿；大杂烩</td><td>a clever pastiche<br>巧妙的模仿之作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel is a clever pastiche of Victorian detective fiction.  这部小说是对维多利亚时代侦探小说的巧妙模仿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salad</td><td>/ˈsæləd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩，混合物；色拉</td><td>a salad of styles<br>风格的大杂烩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The album is a salad of jazz, rock and folk tunes.  这张专辑是爵士、摇滚和民谣的大杂烩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stew</td><td>/ˈstuː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>炖菜；杂糅之物；忧虑</td><td>a stew of styles<br>风格的大杂烩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The building is a stew of styles from three different centuries.  这座建筑是三个不同世纪风格的杂糅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 welter</td><td>/ˈweltər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>混乱，动乱</td><td>a welter of details<br>杂乱的一堆细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report buried its main point in a welter of small details.  这份报告把要点埋在了杂乱的琐碎细节之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>perpetual</b></td><td>/pərˈpetʃuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不断的</td><td>perpetual motion<br>永恒运动，永动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lives in perpetual fear of losing his job.  他一直生活在失业的恐惧中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ceaseless</td><td>/ˈsiːslɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不停的，持续的</td><td>ceaseless noise<br>不停的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceaseless traffic outside made it impossible for us to sleep.  外面不停的车流让我们无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dateless</td><td>/ˈdeɪtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>古老得无从考证的</td><td>dateless folk songs<br>年代不明的民歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These dateless legends were told long before anyone wrote them down.  这些年代不明的传说早在有人记录之前就流传着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deathless</td><td>/ˈdɛθləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不死的，不朽的</td><td>deathless fame<br>不朽的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet won deathless fame with a single slender volume.  这位诗人凭一本薄薄的诗集赢得了不朽名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endless</td><td>/ˈendləs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无尽的，无休止的</td><td>endless complaints<br>无休止的抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The meeting felt endless, though it lasted only ninety minutes.  会议感觉没完没了，其实只开了九十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eternal</td><td>/ɪˈtɜːrnl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>eternal life<br>永生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poem promises the beloved eternal youth.  这首诗许诺爱人永恒的青春。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immortal</td><td>/ɪˈmɔːrtəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不朽的，永生的</td><td>immortal fame<br>不朽的声名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet's immortal lines are still quoted two thousand years later.  这位诗人不朽的诗句两千年后仍被引用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undying</td><td>/ʌnˈdaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不灭的</td><td>undying love<br>永恒的爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She pledged her undying loyalty to the cause she had joined.  她宣誓对所投身的事业永葆忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unending</td><td>/ʌnˈendɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无尽的，不停的</td><td>an unending stream of visitors<br>络绎不绝的来客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An unending stream of visitors filled the museum all summer.  整个夏天络绎不绝的游客挤满了博物馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ephemeral</td><td>/ɪˈfemərəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，转瞬即逝的</td><td>ephemeral fame<br>短暂的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Internet fame is often ephemeral and quickly forgotten.  网络名声往往短暂，很快被遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 evanescent</td><td>/ˌevəˈnesnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，逐渐消失的</td><td>evanescent beauty<br>转瞬即逝的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The evanescent glow of fireflies lit the summer path.  萤火虫短暂的微光照亮了夏夜小径。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 transient</td><td>/ˈtrænʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的；暂时的；过往的</td><td>transient population<br>流动人口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fame in the internet age is often transient and quickly forgotten.  网络时代的名声往往短暂，很快被人遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 transitory</td><td>/ˈtrænsətɔːri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的</td><td>transitory pleasure<br>短暂的快乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His anger was transitory and he apologized within the hour.  他的愤怒很短暂，一小时内就道了歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>persecute</b></td><td>/ˈpɜːrsɪkjuːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>迫害，折磨</td><td>persecute religious minorities<br>迫害宗教少数群体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime persecuted religious minorities for more than a decade.  该政权迫害宗教少数群体十多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agonize</td><td>/ˈæɡənaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）非常痛苦，苦苦思索</td><td>agonize over a decision<br>为决定苦苦纠结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She agonized for weeks over whether to accept the job.  她为是否接受这份工作苦恼了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anguish</td><td>/ˈæŋɡwɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>极度痛苦，苦恼</td><td>in great anguish<br>极度痛苦之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His face showed the anguish of a sleepless night.  他的脸上写着一夜无眠的痛苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 curse</td><td>/ˈkɜːrs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>诅咒，咒骂</td><td>curse one's enemies<br>诅咒自己的敌人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old woman cursed her enemies in a trembling voice.  老妇人用颤抖的声音诅咒她的敌人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 excruciate</del></td><td>/ɪkˈskruːʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦</td><td>excruciate the prisoners<br>折磨囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The interrogators excruciated the prisoners for information they never had.  审讯者折磨囚犯，逼问他们从未掌握的情报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harrow</td><td>/ˈhæroʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>折磨，使苦恼</td><td>harrow his conscience<br>折磨他的良心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The memory of that night harrowed him for the rest of his life.  那一夜的记忆折磨了他的余生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plague</td><td>/pleɪɡ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>瘟疫；折磨，使…痛苦</td><td>plagued by doubts<br>被疑虑折磨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doubts about the decision plagued him for the rest of the year.  对那个决定的疑虑折磨了他余下的一整年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rack</td><td>/ˈræk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦；架子</td><td>racked with pain<br>受痛苦折磨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was racked by guilt long after the incident was forgotten.  事件被淡忘后他仍长久受负罪感折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torment</td><td>/tɔːrˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦；痛苦</td><td>torment the prisoners<br>折磨囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guilt tormented him for years after the accident.  事故之后，负罪感折磨了他多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torture</td><td>/ˈtɔːrtʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，拷打；酷刑</td><td>under torture<br>在酷刑之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long wait for results was pure torture for her.  漫长等待结果对她来说简直是折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>persevere</b></td><td>/ˌpɜːrsəˈvɪr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>坚持不懈，不屈不挠</td><td>persevere in one's efforts<br>坚持不懈地努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She persevered in her research despite years of discouraging results.  尽管多年结果令人沮丧，她仍坚持研究。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carry on</td><td>/ˈkæri ɑn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>继续，坚持下去</td><td>carry on despite setbacks<br>遭挫仍坚持下去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They carried on with the experiment despite repeated failures.  尽管屡屡失败，他们仍继续做实验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endure</td><td>/ɪnˈdʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>忍受；持续存在</td><td>endure great hardship<br>忍受巨大艰苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The settlers endured three winters without proper shelter.  移民们在没有像样住所的情况下熬过了三个冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 persist</td><td>/pərˈsɪst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>持续存在；坚持</td><td>the symptoms persist<br>症状持续</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fog persisted over the runway until late in the afternoon.  雾在跑道上空持续到下午晚些时候。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 renounce</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>正式放弃，声明弃绝</td><td>renounce one's claim<br>声明放弃权利主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He renounced his claim to the inheritance in writing.  他以书面形式放弃了继承权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 succumb</td><td>/səˈkʌm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>屈服；死亡</td><td>succumb to pressure<br>屈服于压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor finally succumbed to pressure and withdrew the unpopular proposal.  市长最终屈服于压力，撤回了不受欢迎的提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 surrender</td><td>/səˈrendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>交出，放弃，投降</td><td>surrender to the police<br>向警方投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief finally surrendered to the police after a long chase.  经过长时间追捕，小偷终于向警方投降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 yield</td><td>/ˈjiːld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>产生，出产；屈服，让出</td><td>yield results<br>产生结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of patient testing finally yielded a usable vaccine.  数月耐心的试验终于产出可用的疫苗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 5

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>persiflage</b></del></td><td>/ˈpɜːrsɪflɑːʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>开玩笑</td><td>idle persiflage<br>无聊的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dinner party was full of idle persiflage and very little substance.  晚宴上尽是无聊的玩笑，几乎没有实质内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 backchat</del></td><td>/ˈbæktʃæt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>顶嘴；打趣的对答</td><td>cheeky backchat<br>无礼的顶嘴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher tolerated no backchat from students during her lessons.  这位老师上课时不容学生顶嘴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 badinage</td><td>/ˌbædənˈɑːʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>打趣，善意的玩笑</td><td>light badinage<br>轻松的打趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their light badinage over dinner kept the whole table laughing.  他们晚餐时轻松的打趣让整桌人笑声不断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chaff</td><td>/tʃæf/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>取笑，善意地嘲弄</td><td>chaff a friend<br>取笑朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His teammates chaffed him about his terrible singing voice.  队友们拿他糟糕的歌喉取笑他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jesting</td><td>/ˈdʒɛstɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>说笑，打趣</td><td>good-humored jesting<br>善意的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their good-humored jesting made the long trip pass quickly.  他们善意的说笑让漫长的旅程过得很快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 Joshing</td><td>/ˈdʒɑʃɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>打趣，开玩笑</td><td>friendly joshing<br>友好的打趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The brothers kept up a friendly joshing all through dinner.  兄弟俩整顿晚餐都在友好地互相打趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 raillery</del></td><td>/ˈreɪləri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>善意的调侃，玩笑</td><td>gentle raillery<br>温和的调侃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He endured her gentle raillery with a patient smile.  他带着耐心的微笑忍受她温和的调侃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repartee</td><td>/ˌrepɑːrˈtiː/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>机智的应答，妙语</td><td>quick repartee<br>机敏的应答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their dinner conversation sparkled with witty repartee that kept every guest laughing.  他们的餐桌交谈妙语连珠，逗得每位客人都在笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>persistence</b></td><td>/pərˈsɪstəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>坚持，持续</td><td>sheer persistence<br>全凭坚持不懈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sheer persistence, not talent, finally won her the research grant.  最终为她赢得研究资助的是坚持不懈，而非天赋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 ceaselessness</del></td><td>/ˈsisləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不间断，无休止</td><td>the ceaselessness of the waves<br>波浪的无休无止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceaselessness of the traffic noise kept him awake all night.  车流噪音无休无止，让他整夜无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 continuance</td><td>/kənˈtɪnjuəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>继续，持续</td><td>the continuance of peace<br>和平的延续</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone hoped for the continuance of the fragile ceasefire.  所有人都希望脆弱的停火能够持续。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 continuity</td><td>/ˌkɑːntəˈnuːəti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>连续性，持续</td><td>ensure continuity of service<br>保证服务的连续</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The handover plan preserves continuity in patient care.  交接方案保证了病患护理的连续性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 durability</td><td>/ˌdʊrəˈbɪləti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>耐久性，持久力</td><td>the durability of the material<br>这种材料的耐久性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Field tests proved the durability of the new leather boots.  实地测试证明了这种新皮靴的耐久性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endurance</td><td>/ɪnˈdʊrəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>忍耐力，耐久力</td><td>physical endurance<br>身体的耐力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long runs slowly built up her endurance over the winter.  长跑在整个冬天慢慢提高了她的耐力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subsistence</td><td>/səbˈsɪstəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>生存，维持生计</td><td>subsistence farming<br>自给性农业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many families here depend on subsistence farming to stay alive.  这里许多家庭靠自给性农业维生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cessation</td><td>/seˈseɪʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>终止，暂停</td><td>a cessation of hostilities<br>停止敌对行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides finally agreed to a cessation of hostilities.  双方最终同意停止敌对行动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 close</td><td>/kloʊz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>关闭；结束</td><td>close the meeting<br>结束会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They closed the discussion before anyone could object.  他们在有人反对之前结束了讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 end</td><td>/ˈend/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>结束；末端</td><td>end the war<br>结束战争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike ended after both sides agreed on wages.  双方就工资达成一致后罢工结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expiration</td><td>/ˌekspəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>到期，终止</td><td>the expiration date<br>到期日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Check the expiration date before you open the sealed package.  打开密封包装前请查看保质期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 finish</td><td>/ˈfɪnɪʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>完成，结束</td><td>finish the report tonight<br>今晚完成报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hopes to finish her thesis before the winter holiday.  她希望在寒假前完成论文。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stoppage</td><td>/ˈstɑːpɪdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>停止，中断</td><td>a work stoppage<br>一次停工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sudden work stoppage halted production for three whole days.  一次突然的停工使生产中断了整整三天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 surcease</del></td><td>/sərˈsiːs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>停止，终止</td><td>without surcease<br>不停地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain fell without surcease for nearly a week.  雨几乎不停地下了近一周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 termination</td><td>/tərməˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>终止，结束</td><td>the termination of the contract<br>合同的终止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Either side may request termination of the agreement early.  任何一方都可要求提前终止协议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>personable</b></td><td>/ˈpɜːrsənəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>风度翩翩的，讨人喜欢的</td><td>a personable young man<br>风度翩翩的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new receptionist is personable and remembers the name of every visitor.  新来的接待员风度翩翩，还记得每位来访者的名字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alluring</td><td>/əˈlʊrɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>诱人的，有魅力的</td><td>an alluring smile<br>迷人的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The alluring aroma of fresh bread drew us into the bakery.  新鲜面包诱人的香气把我们引进了面包店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appealing</td><td>/əˈpiːlɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吸引人的</td><td>an appealing offer<br>有吸引力的提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company made an appealing offer that she could hardly refuse.  公司提出了一个她几乎无法拒绝的诱人条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attractive</td><td>/əˈtræktɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有吸引力的，迷人的</td><td>an attractive offer<br>有吸引力的提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company made him an attractive offer he could not refuse.  公司给了他一个无法拒绝的诱人条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 captivating</td><td>/ˈkæptɪˌveɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迷人的，令人着迷的</td><td>a captivating performance<br>令人着迷的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her captivating voice held the entire audience in complete silence.  她迷人的歌声让全场观众鸦雀无声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 charismatic</td><td>/ˌkærɪzˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有魅力的，有感召力的</td><td>a charismatic leader<br>有感召力的领袖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charismatic speaker convinced thousands to join his new movement.  这位有魅力的演讲者说服数千人加入他的新运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 charming</td><td>/ˈtʃɑːrmɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迷人的，可爱的</td><td>a charming little village<br>迷人的小村庄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent the afternoon in a charming village by the lake.  我们在湖边一个迷人的村庄度过了下午。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comely</td><td>/ˈkʌmli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>秀丽的，好看的</td><td>a comely young woman<br>秀丽的年轻女子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A comely young woman greeted us politely at the gate.  一位秀丽的年轻女子在门口礼貌地迎接我们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enchanting</td><td>/ɪnˈtʃæntɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迷人的，醉人的</td><td>an enchanting melody<br>醉人的旋律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The garden looked enchanting under the soft light of lanterns.  花园在灯笼柔和的光线下显得十分迷人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 handsome</td><td>/ˈhænsəm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>英俊的，好看的</td><td>a handsome young man<br>英俊的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was a handsome man with unusually kind eyes.  他是个英俊的男人，眼神格外和善。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pretty</td><td>/ˈprɪti/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>漂亮的，好看的</td><td>a pretty dress<br>漂亮的裙子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She wore a pretty blue dress to the party.  她穿了一条漂亮的蓝裙子去派对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 grotesque</td><td>/ɡroʊˈtesk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难看的</td><td>a grotesque figure<br>难看的形象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mask was so grotesque that the youngest child began to cry.  那副面具太难看了，最小的孩子哭了起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hideous</td><td>/ˈhɪdiəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非常丑陋的</td><td>a hideous mask<br>丑陋可怕的面具</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They tore down the hideous concrete tower that blocked the view.  他们拆掉了那座挡住视野的丑陋混凝土塔楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ugly</td><td>/ˈʌɡli/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>丑陋的；令人不快的</td><td>an ugly gray facade<br>丑陋的灰色外墙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The building had an ugly gray facade facing the street.  这栋楼临街的一面是丑陋的灰色外墙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>perspicacious</b></td><td>/ˌpɜːrspɪˈkeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有洞察力的，敏锐的</td><td>a perspicacious critic<br>有洞察力的评论家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her perspicacious reading of the market saved the firm millions.  她对市场的敏锐判断为公司省下数百万。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 astute</td><td>/əˈstuːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>机敏的，有洞察力的</td><td>an astute observer<br>敏锐的观察者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An astute observer would have noticed the tension in the room.  一个机敏的观察者会注意到房间里的紧张气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 savvy</td><td>/ˈsævi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>老练，机智；有见识的，精明能干的</td><td>political savvy<br>政治上的老练</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her political savvy helped the bill pass with almost no opposition.  她政治上的老练使那项议案几乎无异议通过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sharp</td><td>/ˈʃɑːrp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖刻的；锋利的；敏锐的</td><td>a sharp tongue<br>说话尖刻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic has a sharp tongue but an unusually fair mind.  这位评论家言辞尖刻，心思却格外公正。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sharp-witted</td><td>/ˈʃɑrp ˈwɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>机敏的，头脑敏锐的</td><td>a sharp-witted reply<br>机敏的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sharp-witted reply silenced the whole panel.  她机敏的回答让整个评审团哑然。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shrewd</td><td>/ʃruːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精明的，机敏的</td><td>a shrewd judgment<br>精明的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was a shrewd judgment that saved the company millions of dollars.  那是个精明的判断，为公司省下了数百万美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smart</td><td>/ˈsmɑːrt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>聪明的；时髦的</td><td>a smart move<br>明智之举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Buying the tickets early was a very smart decision.  提前买票是个非常明智的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ignorant</td><td>/ˈɪɡnərənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知的，不知道的</td><td>ignorant of the facts<br>不了解事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They were completely ignorant of the danger ahead.  他们对前方的危险毫不知情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 oblivious</td><td>/əˈblɪviəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不知道的，不知情的</td><td>oblivious to the noise<br>察觉不到噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Deep in her book, she was oblivious to the noise of the crowd.  她沉浸在书里，察觉不到人群的嘈杂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unknowing</td><td>/ʌnˈnoʊɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不知情的，无意识的</td><td>an unknowing accomplice<br>不知情的帮凶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was an unknowing carrier of the virus for weeks.  她在数周内一直是不知情的病毒携带者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>perspicuity</b></del></td><td>/ˌpɜːrspɪˈkjuːəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>清晰明了</td><td>admirable perspicuity<br>令人称道的明晰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge praised the lawyer for the perspicuity of her written brief.  法官称赞这位律师书面陈述的清晰明了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clarity</td><td>/ˈklærəti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>清晰，清楚</td><td>with great clarity<br>非常清晰地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She explained the complicated rules with surprising clarity.  她把复杂的规则解释得异常清晰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clearness</td><td>/ˈklɪrnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>清晰，明白</td><td>clearness of expression<br>表达的清晰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clearness of her explanation impressed the entire committee.  她解释之清晰让整个委员会印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 explicitness</td><td>/ɪkˈsplɪsɪtnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>明确，清晰明白</td><td>the explicitness of instructions<br>说明的明确性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract's explicitness left no room for later argument.  合同的明确性没给日后争论留下空间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lucidity</td><td>/luˈsɪdɪti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>清晰，明白</td><td>remarkable lucidity<br>非凡的清晰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He explained the theory with surprising lucidity and patience.  他极为清晰而耐心地解释了这个理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 lucidness</del></td><td>/ˈlusɪdnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>清晰，明晰</td><td>the lucidness of prose<br>文章的清晰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers praised the lucidness of her scientific writing.  读者称赞她科学写作的清晰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 perspicuousness</del></td><td>/pərˈspɪkjuəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>清晰易懂</td><td>perspicuousness of style<br>文风的清晰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The textbook is valued for its perspicuousness and useful examples.  这本教材因清晰易懂和实用例子而受重视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 obscureness</del></td><td>/əbˈskjʊrnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>晦涩，不清楚</td><td>the obscureness of his argument<br>论证的晦涩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The obscureness of the wording confused even trained lawyers.  措辞的晦涩连受过训练的律师都困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obscurity</td><td>/əbˈskjʊrəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>晦涩难懂；默默无闻</td><td>live in obscurity<br>默默无闻地生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet died in obscurity but is famous today.  这位诗人默默无闻地去世，如今却很有名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unclarity</del></td><td>/ʌnˈklærəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不清晰，含糊</td><td>unclarity in the rules<br>规则中的含糊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Much of the confusion came from unclarity in the guidelines.  许多混乱源于指南中的含糊之处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pertain</b></td><td>/pərˈteɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>有关联</td><td>pertain to the case<br>与该案件有关</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please bring any documents that pertain to the case tomorrow morning.  请在明天上午带来与该案件有关的任何文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 appertain</del></td><td>/ˌæpərˈteɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>属于；与…有关</td><td>appertain to the office<br>属于该职位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Certain privileges appertain to the office of the mayor.  某些特权属于市长这一职位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bear</td><td>/ˈber/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>承受，支撑；忍受</td><td>bear the weight<br>承受重量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These thin beams cannot bear the weight of the roof.  这些细梁承受不了屋顶的重量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 concern</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>关心，担心；涉及</td><td>show genuine concern<br>表现出真切的关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parents expressed concern about the safety of the playground.  家长们对操场的安全表示关心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refer</td><td>/rəˈfɜːr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>提及；涉及，指的是</td><td>refer to the document<br>提及那份文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer referred to an obscure clause in the contract.  律师提到了合同中一条不显眼的条款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relate</td><td>/rɪˈleɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>与…相关；叙述</td><td>relate to the topic<br>与主题相关</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His question did not relate to the topic under discussion.  他的问题与正在讨论的主题无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
