<tr><td>↳反 ease</td><td>/ˈiːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减轻，缓和；容易</td><td>ease the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine eased her headache within minutes of the first dose.  这药在服下第一剂几分钟内就缓解了她的头痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 palliate</del></td><td>/ˈpælieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓和；掩饰</td><td>palliate the symptoms<br>缓解症状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new drug can only palliate the pain, not cure the disease.  这种新药只能减轻疼痛，无法治愈疾病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relieve</td><td>/rɪˈliːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓解；换班替换</td><td>relieve the pressure<br>缓解压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk relieved the tension in her shoulders.  短暂散步缓解了她肩膀的紧张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pirate</b></td><td>/ˈpaɪrət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>盗版，盗用</td><td>pirate a software program<br>盗版软件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company sued several websites for pirating its accounting software.  该公司起诉了几家盗版其会计软件的网站。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arrogate</td><td>/ˈærəˌɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>擅自占用，霸占</td><td>arrogate power to oneself<br>擅自把权力揽于己身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council arrogated to itself powers belonging to the mayor.  议会擅自把属于市长的权力揽为己有。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commandeer</td><td>/ˌkɑːmənˈdɪr/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强行征用，强占</td><td>commandeer a vehicle<br>强行征用一辆车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soldiers commandeered every truck in the village that morning.  那天早晨士兵们征用了村里所有的卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 convert</td><td>/kənˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改变，转化</td><td>convert heat into energy<br>把热转化为能量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers converted the abandoned warehouse into a bright public library.  工程师们把废弃的仓库改造成了明亮的公共图书馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expropriate</td><td>/eksˈproʊpriˌeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>征用，没收</td><td>expropriate private land<br>征收私人土地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The state expropriated private land to build the new railway.  国家征收私人土地来修建新铁路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preempt</td><td>/priˈempt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预先占有</td><td>preempt the best seats<br>预先占住最好座位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early arrivals had preempted the best seats near the stage.  早到的人已经预先占住了靠舞台的最好座位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 press</td><td>/ˈpres/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>按，压；催促</td><td>press the button<br>按下按钮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He pressed the button twice before the lift moved.  他按了两次按钮电梯才动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seize</td><td>/ˈsiːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>领会，抓住（要点）</td><td>seize the main point<br>抓住要点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good students quickly seize the main point of a difficult lecture.  好学生能迅速抓住难懂讲座的要点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 usurp</td><td>/juːˈzɜːrp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>篡夺，篡位</td><td>usurp the throne<br>篡夺王位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general usurped power after the president fled.  总统逃走后，将军篡夺了权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pithy</b></td><td>/ˈpɪθi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简练的，言简意赅的</td><td>a pithy remark<br>精练的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her pithy summary captured the whole debate in two sentences.  她简练的总结用两句话概括了整场辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 apothegmatic</del></td><td>/ˌæpəθeɡˈmætɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>格言式的，简练精辟的</td><td>an apothegmatic style<br>格言式的文风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His apothegmatic prose fits neatly on a postcard.  他格言式的文字正好能写满一张明信片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brief</td><td>/ˈbriːf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简短的，简洁的</td><td>a brief summary<br>简短的总结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a brief account of the accident to the police.  他向警方简短叙述了事故经过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 capsule</td><td>/ˈkæpsəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>胶囊；压缩的，简要的</td><td>a capsule summary<br>简要概述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor asked for a capsule version of the long report.  编辑要一份长篇报告的浓缩版本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compact</td><td>/ˈkɑːmpækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>契约，协议；紧凑的</td><td>sign a compact<br>签订协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two nations signed a compact to protect the shared river.  两国签订协议保护共有的河流。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compendious</td><td>/kəmˈpɛndiəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简明扼要的</td><td>a compendious reference guide<br>简明扼要的参考手册</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wrote a compendious history of the city in ninety pages.  他用九十页写成了一部简明的城市史。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 concise</td><td>/kənˈsaɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简明的，简洁的</td><td>a concise explanation<br>简明的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Keep your answer concise; the interviewer has only ten minutes.  回答要简洁，面试官只有十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laconic</td><td>/ləˈkɑːnɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简洁（以至于显得粗鲁或难以理解）的</td><td>a laconic reply<br>简短生硬的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His laconic reply told us nothing about what had actually happened.  他简短生硬的回答没有告诉我们实际发生了什么。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 succinct</td><td>/səkˈsɪŋkt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简明的，简洁的</td><td>a succinct summary<br>简洁的总结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her succinct summary covered the entire report in three short paragraphs.  她简洁的总结用三小段概括了整份报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 telegraphic</td><td>/ˌtelɪˈɡræfɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简短扼要的，电报式的</td><td>a telegraphic reply<br>简短扼要的回复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sent a telegraphic note: arriving Tuesday, bring keys.  她发来一条电报式的短信：周二到，带钥匙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 terse</td><td>/tɜːrs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简洁的，简明的</td><td>a terse reply<br>简洁的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His terse note said only that the deal had collapsed.  他简短的字条只说交易破裂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 circuitous</td><td>/sərˈkjuːɪtəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迂回的，不直接的</td><td>a circuitous route<br>迂回的路线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We took a circuitous route to avoid the flooded highway.  我们绕了一条迂回的路以避开被淹的公路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 circumlocutory</del></td><td>/ˌsɜrkəmˈlɑkjətɔri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迂回冗赘的，绕弯子的</td><td>circumlocutory replies<br>绕弯子的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His circumlocutory answers hid a very simple refusal.  他绕弯子的答复掩盖了一个简单的拒绝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 diffuse</td><td>/dɪfˈjuːs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长散漫的；扩散的</td><td>a diffuse and wordy essay<br>散漫冗长的文章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument was far too diffuse to convince a careful reader.  他的论证过于散漫，无法说服细心的读者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rambling</td><td>/ˈræmblɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>漫无目的的，散漫的</td><td>a rambling explanation<br>散漫的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a rambling answer that satisfied nobody present.  他给出散漫的回答，在场无人满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prolix</td><td>/ˈproʊlɪks/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>啰嗦的，冗长的</td><td>prolix legal prose<br>冗长的法律文字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge criticized the prolix brief for burying its argument.  法官批评这份冗长的书状埋没了自己的论点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 verbose</td><td>/vɜːrˈboʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长的，啰嗦的</td><td>a verbose explanation<br>冗长的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her verbose report could have said the same thing in two pages.  她冗长的报告本可以用两页说完同样的内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 windy</td><td>/ˈwɪndi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长的</td><td>a windy speech<br>冗长空洞的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His windy speech said very little in more than an hour.  他冗长的演讲讲了一个多小时却几乎没什么内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wordy</td><td>/ˈwɜːrdi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长的，啰嗦的</td><td>a wordy introduction<br>啰嗦的引言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor cut the wordy opening by half.  编辑把啰嗦的开头删掉一半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pitiless</b></td><td>/ˈpɪtiləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有同情心的</td><td>a pitiless judge<br>冷酷无情的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pitiless judge showed no mercy to the young first offender.  那位冷酷无情的法官对年轻的初犯毫不留情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 affectless</del></td><td>/ˈæfektləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无情感的，冷漠的</td><td>an affectless voice<br>毫无感情的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness described the crime in a flat, affectless tone.  证人用平淡无情感的语调描述这桩罪行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 callous</td><td>/ˈkæləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无同情心的，冷漠的</td><td>a callous remark<br>冷漠无情的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His callous joke about the accident shocked everyone in the room.  他拿这场事故开的冷酷玩笑震惊了房间里的每个人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coldhearted</td><td>/ˈkoʊldˈhɑrtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷酷无情的</td><td>a coldhearted refusal<br>冷酷无情的拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His coldhearted refusal to help shocked even his own family.  他冷酷无情地拒绝帮忙，连自己家人都震惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cruel</td><td>/ˈkruːəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>残酷的，无情的</td><td>a cruel joke<br>残忍的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was cruel to leave the dog out in the storm.  把狗留在暴风雨里太残忍了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harsh</td><td>/hɑːrʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的</td><td>harsh criticism<br>严厉的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge's harsh sentence surprised even the prosecutors in the courtroom.  法官严厉的判决连法庭上的检察官都感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 indurate</del></td><td>/ˈɪndjʊreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变硬；使冷酷无情</td><td>indurate the heart<br>使心变得冷硬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of betrayal had indurated his heart against every plea for sympathy.  多年的背叛使他的心变得冷硬，对任何求情都无动于衷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 merciless</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫无仁慈的，无情的</td><td>a merciless critic<br>毫不留情的批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The merciless summer sun beat down on the empty streets.  无情的夏日阳光炙烤着空荡的街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 charitable</td><td>/ˈtʃærətəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，仁慈的</td><td>a charitable view<br>宽容的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a charitable view of his student's careless mistake.  他对学生的粗心错误持宽容态度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clement</td><td>/ˈklemənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，善良的；气候温和的</td><td>a clement judge<br>宽容的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A clement judge gave the young offender a second chance.  一位宽容的法官给了这名年轻罪犯第二次机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compassionate</td><td>/kəmˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有同情心的，慈悲的</td><td>compassionate care<br>充满关怀的照护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A compassionate nurse sat with the frightened child all night.  一位有同情心的护士整夜陪着那个受惊的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humane</td><td>/hjuˈmeɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>人道的，仁慈的</td><td>humane treatment of prisoners<br>人道地对待囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Activists demanded more humane conditions inside the detention center.  活动人士要求改善拘留中心内的人道条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 merciful</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsɪfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>仁慈的，宽厚的</td><td>a merciful judge<br>宽仁的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court was merciful and gave him only a small fine.  法庭很宽仁，只罚了他少许款项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sympathetic</td><td>/ˌsɪmpəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>同情的，有同情心的</td><td>a sympathetic listener<br>富有同情心的倾听者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse gave her a sympathetic look and held her hand.  护士同情地看着她，握住了她的手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tender</td><td>/ˈtendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>温柔的，心软同情的；正式提出</td><td>a tender heart<br>一颗温柔同情的心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a tender heart and cannot bear to see animals suffer.  她心地温软，见不得动物受苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pittance</b></td><td>/ˈpɪtns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量津贴</td><td>work for a pittance<br>为微薄报酬工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The apprentices worked long hours for a pittance and no benefits.  学徒们长时间工作，只拿微薄津贴且没有福利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bit</td><td>/ˈbɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一点，少量</td><td>a bit of luck<br>一点运气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Add just a bit of salt to the simmering sauce.  往煮着的酱汁里加一点点盐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mite</td><td>/maɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>微小的东西，很少的钱</td><td>a mite of comfort<br>一点点安慰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His brief letter offered a mite of comfort during those difficult winter months.  他简短的信在那些艰难的冬月里带来了一点点安慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 modicum</td><td>/ˈmɑːdɪkəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量</td><td>a modicum of success<br>些许成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 With a modicum of luck they finished the repairs before the storm arrived.  凭着些许运气，他们在暴风雨来临前完成了修理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peanuts</td><td>/ˈpiːnʌts/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>极少的钱，微薄之数</td><td>work for peanuts<br>为微薄报酬工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young interns worked for peanuts and long hours.  年轻实习生拿着微薄报酬长时间工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trace</td><td>/ˈtreɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>痕迹；微量</td><td>a trace of irony<br>一丝讽意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tea showed only a trace of bitterness.  这茶只有一丝苦味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cornucopia</td><td>/ˌkɔːrnjuˈkoʊpiə/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量</td><td>a cornucopia of choices<br>大量的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offered a cornucopia of fresh fruit at surprisingly low prices.  市场上有大量新鲜水果，价格低得惊人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 opulence</td><td>/ˈɑːpjələns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>富裕，丰裕</td><td>live in sheer opulence<br>生活极度奢华</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The palace displayed an opulence that stunned every visiting guest.  这座宫殿展现出令每位来客震惊的奢华。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>placate</b></td><td>/ˈpleɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（通过让步以）平息抚慰</td><td>placate the critics<br>平息批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered full refunds to placate its angriest customers.  公司提供全额退款以平息最愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appease</td><td>/əˈpiːz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安抚，平息；满足</td><td>appease the angry crowd<br>安抚愤怒的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered refunds to appease its angry customers.  公司提供退款以安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conciliate</td><td>/kənˈsɪlieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安抚，平息；调解</td><td>conciliate angry customers<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager tried to conciliate the angry customers with refunds.  经理试图用退款来安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pacify</td><td>/ˈpæsɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使平静，安慰</td><td>pacify a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse sang softly to pacify the frightened child before the injection.  打针前，护士轻声唱歌安抚受惊的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 propitiate</td><td>/prəˈpɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抚慰，劝解</td><td>propitiate the angry gods<br>抚慰愤怒的神灵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers offered rice and flowers to propitiate the mountain spirits.  村民献上米和花来抚慰山神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tranquilize</td><td>/ˈtræŋkwəˌlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使平静，给…服镇静剂</td><td>tranquilize the animal<br>给动物注射镇静剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vet had to tranquilize the frightened horse.  兽医不得不给这匹惊慌的马注射镇静剂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 anger</td><td>/ˈæŋɡər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使愤怒；愤怒</td><td>anger the crowd<br>激怒人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His dismissive remarks angered nearly everyone in the room.  他轻蔑的言论几乎激怒了在场所有人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enrage</td><td>/enˈreɪdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使愤怒，激怒</td><td>enrage the audience<br>激怒观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flippant apology only enraged the families further.  他轻率的道歉只让那些家庭更加愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foment</td><td>/foʊˈment/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>助长，煽动</td><td>foment unrest<br>煽动动乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlets were designed to foment rebellion in the provinces.  这些小册子意在煽动各省叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gall</td><td>/ɡɔːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（使）焦躁，激怒</td><td>galled by the remark<br>被那句话惹恼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His smug tone galled everyone sitting around the conference table.  他自鸣得意的语气激怒了围坐在会议桌旁的每个人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incense</td><td>/ɪnˈsens/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>incense the public<br>激怒公众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal is certain to incense feminists across the country.  这项提案肯定会激怒全国的女权主义者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inflame</td><td>/ɪnˈfleɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使加剧</td><td>inflame public anger<br>使公众愤怒加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's careless comments only inflamed public anger over the housing shortage.  市长轻率的言论只是加剧了公众对住房短缺的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 infuriate</td><td>/ɪnˈfjʊrieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>infuriate the critics<br>激怒批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The decision to cut pensions infuriated thousands of workers across the country.  削减养老金的决定激怒了全国成千上万的工人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 peeve</td><td>/piːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打扰，惹恼</td><td>peeve the audience<br>惹恼观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud phone conversations on the train really peeve the other passengers.  火车上大声打电话确实惹恼其他乘客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rile</td><td>/raɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，惹怒</td><td>rile up the crowd<br>激怒人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing riles him more than being interrupted in the middle of a sentence.  没有什么比话说到一半被打断更让他恼火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>placebo</b></td><td>/pləˈsiːboʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>安慰性的事物</td><td>a mere placebo<br>仅是安慰性的东西</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The promise of a review was a mere placebo for worried residents.  承诺复查对忧心的居民只是安慰性的东西。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>plagiarize</b></td><td>/ˈpleɪdʒəraɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>剽窃，抄袭</td><td>plagiarize another's work<br>抄袭他人的作品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The student was expelled for plagiarizing whole chapters of another thesis.  这名学生因抄袭另一篇论文的整章内容而被开除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 steal</td><td>/ˈstiːl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>潜行，悄悄移动；偷</td><td>steal into the room<br>悄悄溜进房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Each night he would steal quietly out of the house.  他每晚都会悄悄溜出房子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>plangent</b></del></td><td>/ˈplændʒənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（声音）响亮而哀伤的</td><td>a plangent cry<br>哀伤的呼声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plangent cry of a gull echoed across the empty beach.  海鸥凄凉的叫声在空荡的海滩上回响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doleful</td><td>/ˈdoʊlfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲哀的，忧郁的</td><td>a doleful voice<br>悲哀的语调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She told the whole story in a low doleful voice.  她用低沉悲哀的语调讲完了整件事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dolorous</td><td>/ˈdoʊlərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧伤的</td><td>a dolorous sigh<br>忧伤的叹息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A dolorous sigh escaped him whenever the subject of his hometown arose.  每次提到故乡，他都发出忧伤的叹息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 funeral</td><td>/ˈfjuːnərəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>葬礼；哀伤的</td><td>a funeral march<br>一首葬礼进行曲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The organ played a slow funeral march as we entered.  我们进场时管风琴奏起缓慢的葬礼进行曲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grieving</td><td>/ˈɡriːvɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲伤的，哀痛的</td><td>a grieving family<br>悲痛的家人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors brought food to the grieving family each evening.  邻居每晚给悲痛的家人送饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lamentable</td><td>/ləˈmentəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人惋惜的，可悲的</td><td>a lamentable failure<br>令人痛惜的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The condition of the old school building is truly lamentable.  这所旧校舍的状况实在令人惋惜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lugubrious</td><td>/ləˈɡuːbriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（故作夸张的）悲哀的</td><td>a lugubrious expression<br>夸张悲哀的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wore a lugubrious expression whenever anyone mentioned his ruined garden.  每次有人提到他毁掉的花园，他就露出夸张悲哀的表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plaintive</td><td>/ˈpleɪntɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>哀伤的，悲切的</td><td>a plaintive melody<br>哀伤的旋律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A plaintive melody drifted from the open window of the old house.  一段哀伤的旋律从那座老房子敞开的窗子里飘出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rueful</td><td>/ˈruːfəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>悔恨的，懊悔的</td><td>a rueful smile<br>懊悔的苦笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He admitted his error with a rueful smile and a shrug.  他带着懊悔的苦笑和一个耸肩承认了错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sorrowful</td><td>/ˈsɑːroʊfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲伤的，忧伤的</td><td>a sorrowful farewell<br>悲伤的告别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sorrowful eyes told us the news before she spoke.  她悲伤的眼睛在她开口前就告诉了我们消息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 woeful</td><td>/ˈwoʊfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲惨的，糟糕的</td><td>a woeful performance<br>糟糕的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team's woeful defending cost them the championship again.  球队糟糕的防守又一次让他们丢了冠军。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agreeable</td><td>/əˈɡriːəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人愉悦的；相一致的</td><td>an agreeable companion<br>一个讨人喜欢的同伴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She found him an agreeable companion on the long train journey.  她觉得他是长途火车旅程中一位讨人喜欢的同伴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cheerful</td><td>/ˈtʃɪrfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，愉快的</td><td>a cheerful greeting<br>愉快的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed cheerful even after the long delay.  即使长时间延误后他仍保持愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delightful</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪtfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人愉快的，宜人的</td><td>a delightful surprise<br>令人愉快的惊喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent a delightful evening by the lake.  我们在湖边度过了一个愉快的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enjoyable</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒɔɪəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人愉快的，有趣的</td><td>an enjoyable evening<br>愉快的一晚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We had a thoroughly enjoyable evening at the concert.  我们在音乐会上度过了非常愉快的一晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 jolly</td><td>/ˈdʒɑːli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>欢乐的；开玩笑</td><td>a jolly atmosphere<br>欢快的气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jolly innkeeper kept refilling everyone's glass and telling old stories.  这位欢快的店主不停给每人续杯，还讲着旧日的故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pleasing</td><td>/ˈpliːzɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悦耳的，令人愉快的</td><td>a pleasing melody<br>悦耳的旋律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her voice carried a pleasing warmth that calmed the children.  她的声音带着悦耳的温暖，让孩子们安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>plastic</b></td><td>/ˈplæstɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可塑的；易受影响的</td><td>a plastic personality<br>易受塑造的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young children have remarkably plastic minds and absorb new habits quickly.  幼儿的心智极为可塑，能很快吸收新习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adaptable</td><td>/əˈdæptəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>适应力强的；可改编的</td><td>a highly adaptable species<br>适应力极强的物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Crows are adaptable birds that thrive even in crowded cities.  乌鸦适应力很强，连拥挤的城市里也能繁盛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ductile</td><td>/ˈdʌktaɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有延展性的；易受影响的</td><td>ductile metal<br>有延展性的金属</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young recruits are ductile and quickly absorb the habits of the older soldiers.  新兵易受影响，很快就学到老兵的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flexible</td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 malleable</td><td>/ˈmæliəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可塑的</td><td>malleable metals like gold<br>像黄金一样可塑的金属</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gold and other malleable metals can be hammered into thin sheets.  黄金和其他易延展的金属可以被锤成薄片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pliable</td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pliant</td><td>/ˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的；顺从的</td><td>a pliant assistant<br>顺从的助手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted a pliant assistant who would never question his decisions.  他想要一个顺从的助手，永远不会质疑他的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resilient</td><td>/rɪˈzɪljənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有弹性的；能迅速恢复的</td><td>a resilient local economy<br>恢复力强的地方经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children are often more resilient than their parents expect.  孩子往往比父母想象的更有恢复力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supple</td><td>/ˈsʌpl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的</td><td>supple leather<br>柔软的皮革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gloves were made of supple leather that fit her hands perfectly.  这双手套用柔软的皮革制成，和她的手完全贴合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inflexible</td><td>/ˌɪnˈfleksəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不肯变通的，僵化的</td><td>an inflexible rule<br>不容变通的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager was inflexible about the submission deadline.  经理在提交截止日期上毫不通融。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rigid</td><td>/ˈrɪdʒəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，僵硬的</td><td>rigid discipline<br>严格的纪律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school enforces rigid rules about mobile phones.  学校对手机执行严格的规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stiff</td><td>/stɪf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵硬的，无法弯曲的；艰苦的，费力的</td><td>a stiff leather collar<br>硬挺的皮质衣领</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stiff leather collar rubbed his neck raw during the long march.  长途行军中，硬挺的皮领把他的脖子磨破了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>plateau</b></td><td>/plæˈtoʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>高原；稳定时期，平台期</td><td>reach a plateau<br>进入平台期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales rose sharply for a year and then reached a plateau.  销售额猛涨了一年，随后进入平台期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 highland</td><td>/ˈhaɪlənd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>高地，山地</td><td>the Scottish highlands<br>苏格兰高地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sheep grazed across the windswept highlands all through summer.  整个夏天羊群都在风大的高地上放牧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mesa</td><td>/ˈmeɪsə/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>平顶山，台地</td><td>a flat-topped mesa<br>一座平顶台地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A flat-topped mesa rose sharply above the desert floor.  一座平顶台地从沙漠地面陡然升起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tableland</td><td>/ˈteɪbəlˌlænd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>高原，台地</td><td>a barren tableland<br>荒芜的高原</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The road climbed for hours onto a barren tableland.  道路盘旋数小时才登上一片荒芜的高原。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 upland</td><td>/ˈʌplənd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>高地，山地</td><td>upland pasture<br>高地牧场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers move their cattle to upland pasture every June.  农民每年六月把牛群赶到高地牧场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equilibrium</td><td>/ˌiːkwɪˈlɪbriəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>均势，平衡</td><td>reach a stable equilibrium<br>达到稳定的平衡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market took two years to reach a stable equilibrium again.  市场花了两年才重新达到稳定的平衡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stage</td><td>/ˈsteɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>阶段，时期</td><td>an early stage<br>早期阶段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project is still at an early stage of planning.  这个项目仍处在规划的早期阶段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stasis</td><td>/ˈsteɪsɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>停滞，静止状态</td><td>a period of stasis<br>一段停滞时期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The industry entered a long stasis after the financial crisis.  金融危机后该行业进入长期停滞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lowland</td><td>/ˈloʊˌlænd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>低地，平原</td><td>fertile lowland fields<br>肥沃的低地田地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rice grows best in the fertile lowland fields near the river.  水稻在河边肥沃的低地田里长得最好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>platitude</b></td><td>/ˈplætɪtuːd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>陈词滥调</td><td>utter a platitude<br>说一句陈词滥调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speech offered nothing but platitudes about hard work and dreams.  这篇演讲除了关于努力与梦想的陈词滥调外别无他物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 banality</td><td>/bəˈnæləti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>平庸；陈腐的话</td><td>the banality of his speech<br>他讲话的平庸乏味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film offered nothing but banalities about growing up.  这部电影只提供了些关于成长的陈腐说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bromide</td><td>/ˈbroʊmaɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>陈词滥调；镇静剂</td><td>a tired old bromide<br>老掉牙的陈词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech ended with a tired old bromide about hard work.  他的演讲以一句关于努力工作的陈腐套话作结。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cliché</del></td><td>/kliːˈʃeɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>陈词滥调，老套的说法</td><td>an overused cliché<br>用滥的陈词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Saying that time heals everything has become a tired cliché.  说时间治愈一切已成为老套的陈词滥调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commonplace</td><td>/ˈkɑːmənpleɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>平凡的；老生常谈</td><td>a commonplace observation<br>老生常谈的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Video calls have become commonplace in ordinary office life.  视频通话在日常办公中已很平常。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 homily</td><td>/ˈhɑːməli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>冗长乏味的道德讲演或训诫</td><td>deliver a homily<br>作一番训诫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We had to sit through another homily about punctuality and respect.  我们只得又听了一通关于守时与尊重的冗长训诫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shibboleth</td><td>/ˈʃɪbəˌleθ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>陈腐的口号；标志性用语</td><td>an outworn shibboleth<br>过时的老口号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Free trade became a shibboleth repeated by every candidate.  自由贸易成了每位候选人都挂在嘴上的口号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 truism</td><td>/ˈtruːɪzəm/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>陈词滥调</td><td>a familiar truism<br>一句熟悉的老话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is a truism that hard work alone rarely guarantees success.  仅靠努力很少能保证成功，这是句老生常谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>plausible</b></td><td>/ˈplɔːzəbl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可信的</td><td>a plausible explanation<br>一个可信的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a plausible explanation, though nobody could actually verify it.  他给出了一个可信的解释，尽管无人能真正核实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 believable</td><td>/bəˈliːvəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可信的，可相信的</td><td>a believable character<br>可信的人物形象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel succeeds because every character feels entirely believable.  这部小说之所以成功，是因为每个人物都让人完全信服。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 credible</td><td>/ˈkredəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可信的，可靠的</td><td>a credible witness<br>可信的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prosecution produced a credible witness at the last moment.  检方在最后一刻找出了一名可信的证人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 likely</td><td>/ˈlaɪkli/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>可能的；看来可信的</td><td>the likely explanation<br>看来可信的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The most likely explanation is simple human error.  最可能的解释就是简单的人为失误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 possible</td><td>/ˈpɑːsəbəl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可能的，或许存在的</td><td>one possible answer<br>一个可能的答案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is one possible answer nobody has considered yet.  有一个可能的答案还没人想到过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presumable</td><td>/prɪˈzuməbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可推测的，大概如此的</td><td>the presumable cause<br>可推测的原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fatigue was the presumable cause of the driver's mistake.  疲劳大概是司机失误的原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 probable</td><td>/ˈprɑːbəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>很可能的，有可能的</td><td>the probable outcome<br>很可能的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A narrow victory seems the probable outcome of the election.  小幅胜出似乎是这次选举很可能的结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 improbable</td><td>/ˌɪmˈprɑːbəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不大可能的，难以置信的</td><td>a highly improbable story<br>极不可能的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He told a highly improbable story about losing the keys.  他讲了个极不可能的丢钥匙的故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incredible</td><td>/ˌɪnˈkredəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以置信的</td><td>an incredible excuse<br>难以置信的借口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She offered an incredible excuse that nobody in the room believed.  她给出一个难以置信的借口，屋里没人相信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unbelievable</td><td>/ˌʌnbəˈliːvəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法相信的</td><td>an unbelievable claim<br>无法相信的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unbelievable claim about the treasure amused the whole village.  他关于宝藏的荒唐说法逗乐了整个村子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unlikely</td><td>/ʌnˈlaɪkli/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可能的，未必的</td><td>an unlikely story<br>不可信的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seems unlikely that the plan will be approved.  这个计划看来不太可能获批。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>plague</b></td><td>/pleɪɡ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>瘟疫；折磨，使…痛苦</td><td>plagued by doubts<br>被疑虑折磨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doubts about the decision plagued him for the rest of the year.  对那个决定的疑虑折磨了他余下的一整年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pestilence</td><td>/ˈpestələns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>瘟疫，疫病</td><td>a deadly pestilence<br>致命的瘟疫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A deadly pestilence swept the city during that dreadful summer.  那个可怕的夏天，一场致命瘟疫扫过全城。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agonize</td><td>/ˈæɡənaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）非常痛苦，苦苦思索</td><td>agonize over a decision<br>为决定苦苦纠结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She agonized for weeks over whether to accept the job.  她为是否接受这份工作苦恼了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anguish</td><td>/ˈæŋɡwɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>极度痛苦，苦恼</td><td>in great anguish<br>极度痛苦之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His face showed the anguish of a sleepless night.  他的脸上写着一夜无眠的痛苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 excruciate</del></td><td>/ɪkˈskruːʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦</td><td>excruciate the prisoners<br>折磨囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The interrogators excruciated the prisoners for information they never had.  审讯者折磨囚犯，逼问他们从未掌握的情报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harrow</td><td>/ˈhæroʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>折磨，使苦恼</td><td>harrow his conscience<br>折磨他的良心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The memory of that night harrowed him for the rest of his life.  那一夜的记忆折磨了他的余生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 persecute</td><td>/ˈpɜːrsɪkjuːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>迫害，折磨</td><td>persecute religious minorities<br>迫害宗教少数群体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime persecuted religious minorities for more than a decade.  该政权迫害宗教少数群体十多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 afflict</td><td>/əˈflɪkt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使痛苦，折磨</td><td>be afflicted with doubt<br>被疑虑折磨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drought has afflicted the region for three straight years.  干旱已连续三年折磨这个地区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torment</td><td>/tɔːrˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦；痛苦</td><td>torment the prisoners<br>折磨囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guilt tormented him for years after the accident.  事故之后，负罪感折磨了他多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torture</td><td>/ˈtɔːrtʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，拷打；酷刑</td><td>under torture<br>在酷刑之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long wait for results was pure torture for her.  漫长等待结果对她来说简直是折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>poignant</b></td><td>/ˈpɔɪnjənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人感伤的</td><td>a poignant reminder<br>令人感伤的提醒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old photograph was a poignant reminder of everything they had lost.  那张旧照片令人感伤地提醒着他们失去的一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affecting</td><td>/əˈfektɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>动人的，令人感动的</td><td>an affecting story<br>一个动人的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She told an affecting story about her grandmother's last letter.  她讲了一个关于祖母最后一封信的动人故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impactful</td><td>/ɪmˈpæktfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有强烈影响的，震撼的</td><td>an impactful ending<br>震撼人心的结尾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary's impactful ending stayed with viewers for weeks.  这部纪录片震撼的结尾让观众记了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impressive</td><td>/ɪmˈpresɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人印象深刻的</td><td>an impressive performance<br>令人印象深刻的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her command of four languages is genuinely impressive.  她掌握四门语言的能力确实令人印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moving</td><td>/ˈmuːvɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>感人的，动人的</td><td>a moving tribute<br>一段感人的悼词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His moving tribute to the old teacher silenced the hall.  他对老教师感人的悼念让全场静默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 touching</td><td>/ˈtʌtʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>介词、形容词</td><td>关于，涉及；动人的</td><td>touching the question of cost<br>关于成本问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The letter said little touching the terms of payment.  信中几乎没有提及付款条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unaffecting</del></td><td>/ˌʌnəˈfɛktɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不动人的，无感染力的</td><td>a flat, unaffecting performance<br>平淡无感染力的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play was competent but oddly unaffecting throughout.  这部剧演得规矩，却全程出奇地缺乏感染力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unemotional</td><td>/ˌʌnɪˈmoʊʃənəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不动感情的，冷静的</td><td>an unemotional account<br>不带感情的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unemotional summary of the disaster unsettled the listeners.  她不带感情的灾难总结让听众不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unimpressive</td><td>/ˌʌnɪmˈpresɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平淡无奇的，不起眼的</td><td>an unimpressive debut<br>平淡的首次登场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unimpressive debut gave no hint of the fame ahead.  他平淡的首次登场丝毫看不出日后的名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>plethora</b></td><td>/ˈpleθərə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>过量，过剩</td><td>a plethora of options<br>过多的选项</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The menu offers a plethora of options that confuse first time visitors.  菜单提供过多的选项，让初次光顾的客人不知所选。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abundance</td><td>/əˈbʌndəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>丰富，大量</td><td>an abundance of fresh fruit<br>大量新鲜水果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offered an abundance of fresh fruit at low prices.  市场以低价供应大量新鲜水果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cornucopia</td><td>/ˌkɔːrnjuˈkoʊpiə/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量</td><td>a cornucopia of choices<br>大量的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offered a cornucopia of fresh fruit at surprisingly low prices.  市场上有大量新鲜水果，价格低得惊人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feast</td><td>/ˈfiːst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>盛宴；丰盛的享受</td><td>a feast of colors<br>色彩的盛宴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The autumn hillside offered a feast of colors to hikers.  秋日的山坡为徒步者献上一场色彩的盛宴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overabundance</td><td>/ˌoʊvərəˈbʌndəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>过多，过剩</td><td>an overabundance of caution<br>过度的谨慎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An overabundance of rain ruined most of the wheat crop.  雨水过多毁掉了大部分小麦收成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overflow</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈfloʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>溢出，泛滥</td><td>overflow the river banks<br>河水漫过堤岸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Melting snow made the river overflow its banks last spring.  去年春天融雪使河水漫过堤岸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 plentitude</del></td><td>/ˈplɛnɪtud/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>充足，大量</td><td>a plentitude of talent<br>人才济济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The region enjoys a plentitude of water and fertile soil.  该地区拥有充足的水源和肥沃的土壤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profusion</td><td>/prəˈfjuːʒn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>丰富，大量</td><td>a profusion of flowers<br>繁花盛开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A profusion of wildflowers covered the hillside after the spring rains.  春雨过后，山坡上开满了大量野花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surfeit</td><td>/ˈsɜːrfɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（使）过量，（使）饮食过度</td><td>a surfeit of information<br>信息过量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A surfeit of information often leaves readers more confused than informed.  信息过量常让读者更困惑而非更明白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surplus</td><td>/ˈsɜːrpləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>过剩，剩余</td><td>a grain surplus<br>粮食过剩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country exported its grain surplus to neighboring markets.  该国把过剩的粮食出口到邻国市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dearth</td><td>/dɜːrθ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>供应不足</td><td>a dearth of talent<br>人才匮乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a real dearth of affordable housing in this district.  这个区确实缺乏买得起的住房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inadequacy</td><td>/ɪnˈædɪkwəsi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不足，能力不够</td><td>a sense of inadequacy<br>能力不足感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report exposed the inadequacy of existing safety rules.  报告揭示了现有安全规定的不足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insufficiency</td><td>/ˌɪnsəˈfɪʃənsiː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不足，不充分</td><td>insufficiency of evidence<br>证据不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The case was dismissed because of the insufficiency of the evidence.  由于证据不足，案件被驳回。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 paucity</td><td>/ˈpɔːsəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量，缺乏</td><td>a paucity of data<br>数据匮乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paucity of reliable data made the researchers cautious about conclusions.  可靠数据的匮乏使研究者对结论十分谨慎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scarcity</td><td>/ˈskersɪti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>稀缺，不足</td><td>a scarcity of housing<br>住房稀缺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A scarcity of clean water threatens millions of people every year.  清洁水源的稀缺每年威胁数百万人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 undersupply</del></td><td>/ˌʌndərsəˈplaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>供应不足</td><td>an undersupply of nurses<br>护士供应不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A chronic undersupply of nurses has strained the country's hospitals.  护士长期供应不足使全国医院不堪重负。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pliable</b></td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adaptable</td><td>/əˈdæptəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>适应力强的；可改编的</td><td>a highly adaptable species<br>适应力极强的物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Crows are adaptable birds that thrive even in crowded cities.  乌鸦适应力很强，连拥挤的城市里也能繁盛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ductile</td><td>/ˈdʌktaɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有延展性的；易受影响的</td><td>ductile metal<br>有延展性的金属</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young recruits are ductile and quickly absorb the habits of the older soldiers.  新兵易受影响，很快就学到老兵的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flexible</td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 limber</td><td>/ˈlɪmbər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔软灵活的</td><td>stay limber and loose<br>保持柔软放松</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dancers stretch every morning to stay limber and avoid painful injuries.  舞者每天早上拉伸，以保持柔软并避免疼痛的伤病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plastic</td><td>/ˈplæstɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可塑的；易受影响的</td><td>a plastic personality<br>易受塑造的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young children have remarkably plastic minds and absorb new habits quickly.  幼儿的心智极为可塑，能很快吸收新习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supple</td><td>/ˈsʌpl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的</td><td>supple leather<br>柔软的皮革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gloves were made of supple leather that fit her hands perfectly.  这双手套用柔软的皮革制成，和她的手完全贴合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obedient</td><td>/əˈbiːdiənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>an obedient child<br>听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog sat quietly, obedient to every hand signal.  那只狗安静地坐着，服从每个手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pliant</td><td>/ˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的；顺从的</td><td>a pliant assistant<br>顺从的助手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted a pliant assistant who would never question his decisions.  他想要一个顺从的助手，永远不会质疑他的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tractable</td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subdued</td><td>/səbˈduːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的；柔和低沉的</td><td>in a subdued voice<br>用压低的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd grew subdued once the announcement was finally made.  消息宣布后，人群变得安静顺从。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inflexible</td><td>/ˌɪnˈfleksəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不肯变通的，僵化的</td><td>an inflexible rule<br>不容变通的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager was inflexible about the submission deadline.  经理在提交截止日期上毫不通融。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rigid</td><td>/ˈrɪdʒəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，僵硬的</td><td>rigid discipline<br>严格的纪律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school enforces rigid rules about mobile phones.  学校对手机执行严格的规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stiff</td><td>/stɪf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵硬的，无法弯曲的；艰苦的，费力的</td><td>a stiff leather collar<br>硬挺的皮质衣领</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stiff leather collar rubbed his neck raw during the long march.  长途行军中，硬挺的皮领把他的脖子磨破了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unbending</td><td>/ʌnˈbendɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不亲切的；不妥协的</td><td>an unbending attitude<br>不肯通融的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old teacher was unbending about rules and manners.  这位老教师在规矩和礼节上毫不通融。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 contumacious</del></td><td>/ˌkɑːntuˈmeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，倔强的</td><td>a contumacious witness<br>不服从的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contumacious witness was fined for defying the judge.  那名抗命的证人因违抗法官而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insubordinate</td><td>/ɪnsəˈbɔːrdənət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，抗命的</td><td>insubordinate behavior<br>抗命的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was discharged for insubordinate conduct at sea.  这名军官因在海上抗命行为被开除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obstreperous</td><td>/əbˈstrepərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不听话的，任性的</td><td>an obstreperous child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obstreperous child kept kicking the seat in front of him.  一个吵闹不驯的孩子一直踢着前排的座椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recalcitrant</td><td>/rɪˈkælsɪtrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽抗的，不顺从的</td><td>a recalcitrant employee<br>不听劝的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A recalcitrant employee refused every instruction his new supervisor gave him.  一名顽抗的员工拒绝了新主管给他的每一条指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>

<tr><td>↳反 unruly</td><td>/ʌnˈruːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难驾驭的，不守规矩的</td><td>an unruly crowd<br>不守规矩的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teachers struggled to calm the unruly class.  两位老师费力地安抚那个不守规矩的班级。</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obstreperous</td><td>/əbˈstrepərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不听话的，任性的</td><td>an obstreperous child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obstreperous child kept kicking the seat in front of him.  一个吵闹不驯的孩子一直踢着前排的座椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recalcitrant</td><td>/rɪˈkælsɪtrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽抗的，不顺从的</td><td>a recalcitrant employee<br>不听劝的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A recalcitrant employee refused every instruction his new supervisor gave him.  一名顽抗的员工拒绝了新主管给他的每一条指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unruly</td><td>/ʌnˈruːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难驾驭的，不守规矩的</td><td>an unruly crowd<br>不守规矩的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teachers struggled to calm the unruly class.  两位老师费力地安抚那个不守规矩的班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>plight</b></td><td>/plaɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>困境</td><td>the plight of refugees<br>难民的困境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary drew attention to the plight of refugees along the border.  这部纪录片让人们关注边境难民的困境。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dilemma</td><td>/dɪˈlemə/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>困境，两难处境</td><td>face a painful dilemma<br>面临痛苦的两难</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She faced the dilemma of staying home or taking the job.  她面临留在家里还是接受工作的两难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jam</td><td>/ˈdʒæm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>困境，窘境；堵塞</td><td>be in a real jam<br>处境十分为难</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We were in a real jam when the car broke down.  车坏了的时候我们真的很为难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predicament</td><td>/prɪˈdɪkəmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>困境，窘境</td><td>a serious predicament<br>严重的窘境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Losing his passport abroad left him in a serious predicament.  在国外丢了护照让他陷入严重困境。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quandary</td><td>/ˈkwɑːndəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>困惑，窘境</td><td>in a real quandary<br>陷入真正的困境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The offer of two jobs left him in a real quandary.  两份工作的邀约让他陷入真正的困境。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>plodding</b></td><td>/ˈplɑːdɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无聊的，单调乏味的</td><td>a plodding narrative<br>单调乏味的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel has a plodding narrative that tests the patience of readers.  这部小说叙述单调乏味，考验读者的耐心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dreary</td><td>/ˈdrɪri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调乏味的</td><td>a dreary afternoon<br>沉闷的下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent a dreary afternoon indoors watching rain streak the windows.  我们在室内度过一个沉闷的下午，看着雨水划过窗玻璃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dull</td><td>/dʌl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的；乏味的</td><td>a dull student<br>迟钝的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecture was so dull that half the class slept.  讲座乏味得让一半学生睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 humdrum</td><td>/ˈhʌmdrʌm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调乏味的；平淡的</td><td>a humdrum routine<br>单调乏味的日常</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She left the humdrum job to travel and write full time.  她辞掉那份单调乏味的工作，去全职旅行和写作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jejune</td><td>/dʒɪˈdʒuːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣乏味的</td><td>a jejune argument<br>乏味浅薄的论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics dismissed the sequel as jejune and repetitive.  评论家认为这部续集乏味且重复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 monotone</td><td>/ˈmɑːnəˌtoʊn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>单调的声音；单调的</td><td>speak in a monotone<br>用单调的语调说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecturer read his notes in a flat monotone.  讲师用平板单调的语调念稿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 monotonous</td><td>/məˈnɑːtənəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调的，无变化的</td><td>a monotonous voice<br>单调的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His monotonous voice made the afternoon lecture almost impossible to sit through.  他单调的声音让下午的讲座几乎难以听完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pedestrian</td><td>/pəˈdestriən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平庸无奇的，令人厌倦的</td><td>a pedestrian style<br>平庸的文风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reviewer called the plot pedestrian and the dialogue painfully flat.  评论者称其情节平庸，对话乏味得令人难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stale</td><td>/steɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈旧乏味的</td><td>stale jokes<br>老掉牙的笑话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His stale jokes made the audience shift restlessly in their seats.  他老掉牙的笑话让观众在座位上不安地挪动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stodgy</td><td>/ˈstɑːdʒi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>古板守旧的，乏味的</td><td>a stodgy old textbook<br>一本乏味的旧教材</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The course relied on a stodgy old textbook nobody enjoyed reading.  这门课用的是一本没人爱读的乏味旧教材。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absorbing</td><td>/əbˈzɔːrbɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，吸引人的</td><td>an absorbing novel<br>引人入胜的小说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I found her account of the voyage utterly absorbing.  我觉得她对航行的叙述极其引人入胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 engaging</td><td>/ɪnˈɡeɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有吸引力的，讨人喜欢的</td><td>an engaging personality<br>讨人喜欢的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His engaging manner made strangers feel comfortable within minutes.  他讨人喜欢的举止让陌生人几分钟内就放松下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 engrossing</td><td>/ɪnˈɡroʊsɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，令人专注的</td><td>an engrossing story<br>引人入胜的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary was so engrossing that nobody left early.  这部纪录片如此引人入胜，没人提早离场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gripping</td><td>/ˈɡrɪpɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>扣人心弦的，紧张的</td><td>a gripping thriller<br>扣人心弦的惊悚片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final chapter is gripping from start to finish.  最后一章从头到尾都扣人心弦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 interesting</td><td>/ˈɪntrəstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有趣的，引人入胜的</td><td>an interesting proposal<br>一个有趣的提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She raised an interesting objection nobody had anticipated.  她提出了一个无人预料到的有趣反对意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intriguing</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtriːɡɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人好奇的，有趣的</td><td>an intriguing question<br>引人好奇的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She raised an intriguing point that nobody had considered.  她提出了一个无人考虑过的有趣观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 involving</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlvɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，扣人心弦的</td><td>an involving story<br>引人入胜的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary is more involving than any thriller this year.  这部纪录片比今年任何惊悚片都更扣人心弦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 riveting</td><td>/ˈrɪvɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吸引人的，极迷人的</td><td>a riveting account<br>引人入胜的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary offers a riveting account of life inside the mountain villages.  这部纪录片对山村生活的叙述引人入胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pluck</b></td><td>/plʌk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>敢于面对困难的勇气</td><td>show great pluck<br>表现出极大勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took real pluck to speak up against the whole committee.  敢于当众反对整个委员会需要真正的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 backbone</td><td>/ˈbækˌboʊn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>骨气，勇气；脊骨</td><td>show some backbone<br>表现出些骨气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took real backbone to challenge his own boss publicly.  公开质疑自己的老板需要真正的骨气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 courage</td><td>/ˈkɜːrədʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>勇气，胆量</td><td>summon up courage<br>鼓起勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took real courage to admit the mistake in front of everyone.  在众人面前承认错误需要真正的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 dauntlessness</del></td><td>/ˈdɔːntləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无畏，大胆</td><td>the dauntlessness of youth<br>年轻人的无畏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His dauntlessness in the storm saved the whole crew.  他在风暴中的无畏救了全体船员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grit</td><td>/ɡrɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（面对困难时所表现出来的）毅力</td><td>sheer grit and patience<br>纯粹的毅力与耐心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took real grit to finish the race on an injured ankle.  脚踝受伤还跑完全程，靠的是真正的毅力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 guts</td><td>/ɡʌts/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>勇气，胆量；内脏</td><td>have the guts<br>有胆量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had the guts to quit and start her own company.  她有胆量辞职创办自己的公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resolution</td><td>/ˌrezəˈluːʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>决心，坚定；决议</td><td>act with resolution<br>果断坚定地行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She faced that difficult year with quiet resolution.  她以沉静的决心面对那艰难的一年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spirit</td><td>/ˈspɪrət/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>勇气，志气；精神</td><td>show real spirit<br>表现出真正的骨气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small team played with spirit until the final whistle.  这支小队斗志昂扬地打到终场哨响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spunk</td><td>/spʌŋk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>勇气，胆量</td><td>have the spunk to refuse<br>有胆量拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took real spunk to speak against her own boss.  当面反对自己的老板需要真正的胆量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cowardice</td><td>/ˈkaʊərdɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>怯懦，胆小</td><td>an act of cowardice<br>怯懦之举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Refusing to speak up for a friend struck him later as cowardice.  没有为朋友说话，事后他觉得那是怯懦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 coward</td><td>/ˈkaʊərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>懦夫，胆小者</td><td>call him a coward<br>骂他是懦夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody dared call him a coward after that rescue.  那次救援之后没人敢说他是懦夫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 spinelessness</del></td><td>/ˈspaɪnləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>没骨气，软弱</td><td>moral spinelessness<br>道德上的软弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His spinelessness in meetings meant unfair decisions were never challenged.  他在会上没骨气，导致不公的决定从未被质疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>plumb</b></td><td>/plʌm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>完全的；深入探究，探测</td><td>plumb the depths of<br>深入探究…的深处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The biographer tried to plumb the depths of that private grief.  传记作者试图深入探究那份私人悲伤的深处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 absolute</td><td>/ˈæbsəluːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>专制的；无限的；纯净不掺杂的；确凿的</td><td>absolute power<br>绝对的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The king held absolute power over his subjects.  国王对臣民拥有绝对权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 categorical</td><td>/ˌkætəˈɡɔːrɪkl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有例外的；绝对的</td><td>a categorical denial<br>断然的否认</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister issued a categorical denial of the corruption charges.  部长断然否认了腐败指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complete</td><td>/kəmˈpliːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>完全的，彻底的；完成</td><td>a complete failure<br>彻底的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The first attempt was a complete failure.  第一次尝试彻底失败了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consummate</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsəmət/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>专业的，有造诣的；无纰漏的，完美的</td><td>a consummate professional<br>极为专业的行家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She handled the hostile interview with the ease of a consummate diplomat.  她像老练的外交家一样从容应对了这场充满敌意的采访。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 definite</td><td>/ˈdefənət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确定的</td><td>a definite answer<br>明确的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We need a definite answer before we can book the venue.  我们需要明确答复才能订场地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pure</td><td>/ˈpjʊr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>纯粹的，完全的</td><td>pure coincidence<br>纯属巧合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Meeting her there was pure coincidence, nothing more.  在那里遇见她纯属巧合，别无其他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sheer</td><td>/ˈʃɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全的，纯粹的</td><td>sheer nonsense<br>纯粹的胡说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What he wrote about the treaty was sheer nonsense.  他关于那份条约写的全是胡说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thorough</td><td>/ˈθɜːroʊ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>彻底的，详尽的</td><td>a thorough investigation<br>彻底的调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Inspectors carried out a thorough search of every storage room.  检查员对每间储藏室都做了彻底搜查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 utter</td><td>/ˈʌtər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>完全的，彻底的；说出</td><td>utter nonsense<br>一派胡言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What he said was utter nonsense from start to finish.  他说的从头到尾都是彻底的胡话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 very</td><td>/ˈveri/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，正是那个的</td><td>the very top<br>最顶端处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They climbed to the very top of the ruined tower.  他们爬到了那座残塔的最顶端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 explore</td><td>/ɪksˈplɔːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>探索，深入研究</td><td>explore every possibility<br>探究每一种可能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee explored every possibility before making its decision.  委员会在做决定前探究了每一种可能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delve</td><td>/delv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>探究，钻研</td><td>delve into ancient records<br>钻研古代记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She delved into the family archives and found a startling old letter.  她钻研家族档案，发现了一封令人震惊的旧信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inquire</td><td>/ɪnˈkwaɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>询问，查问</td><td>inquire about the price<br>询问价格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She inquired politely about the train's departure time.  她礼貌地询问列车发车时间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 investigate</td><td>/ɪnˈvestɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>调查，研究</td><td>investigate the cause thoroughly<br>彻底调查原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police are investigating the cause of the warehouse fire.  警方正在调查仓库火灾的原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 probe</td><td>/proʊb/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>深入调查</td><td>probe deeply into the scandal<br>深入调查这桩丑闻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investigators continued to probe deeply into the company's missing funds.  调查人员继续深入调查公司资金失踪的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 doubtful</td><td>/ˈdaʊtfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以确定的，可疑的</td><td>of doubtful value<br>价值可疑的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is doubtful whether the bridge can carry such loads.  这座桥能否承受如此重量还很难说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dubious</td><td>/ˈduːbiəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满不定性的，值得怀疑的</td><td>a dubious claim<br>可疑的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The advertisement made a dubious claim that no serious scientist would support.  那则广告作出了一个没有严肃科学家会支持的可疑说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 equivocal</td><td>/ɪˈkwɪvəkəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模棱两可的，含糊的</td><td>an equivocal answer<br>模棱两可的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness gave an equivocal account of that evening.  证人对那晚的叙述模棱两可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 questionable</td><td>/ˈkwestʃənəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可疑的，靠不住的</td><td>questionable evidence<br>可疑的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The deal rested on questionable accounting practices.  这笔交易建立在可疑的会计做法上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uncertain</td><td>/ʌnˈsɜːrtn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不确定的，不明朗的</td><td>an uncertain future<br>不明朗的未来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The date of the meeting remains uncertain until funding is approved.  在资金获批前会议日期仍不确定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>plummet</b></td><td>/ˈplʌmɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>突然下降</td><td>plummet to a record low<br>暴跌至历史低点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Share prices plummeted to a record low after the announcement.  公告发布后，股价暴跌至历史低点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crash</td><td>/ˈkræʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>暴跌；撞毁</td><td>share prices crash<br>股价暴跌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Share prices crashed within hours of the announcement.  公告发布后几小时内股价暴跌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decline</td><td>/dɪˈklaɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝；下降，衰退</td><td>decline an invitation<br>谢绝邀请</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She politely declined the invitation to speak at the ceremony.  她礼貌地谢绝了在典礼上发言的邀请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 descend</td><td>/dɪˈsend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>下降；世代相传</td><td>descend from the summit<br>从山顶下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path descends steeply toward the river through thick pine forest.  小径穿过浓密的松林，向河边陡然下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dip</td><td>/ˈdɪp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下降，下沉；蘸</td><td>a dip in sales<br>销量的下滑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Temperatures dip sharply once the sun sets over the desert.  沙漠上日落后气温骤降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dive</td><td>/ˈdaɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>骤降；跳水，俯冲</td><td>dive sharply<br>急剧下跌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Profits dived sharply after the factory fire last spring.  去年春天工厂火灾后利润急剧下跌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fall</td><td>/ˈfɔːl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下降，落下；秋天</td><td>a sharp fall in prices<br>价格急剧下跌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Share prices fell again after the disappointing earnings report.  令人失望的财报公布后股价再次下跌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plunge</td><td>/plʌndʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>突然下降</td><td>plunge into the valley<br>骤降到谷底</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The road plunges into the valley just beyond the last farmhouse.  过了最后一座农舍，道路便骤降进山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sink</td><td>/ˈsɪŋk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下沉，下降；水槽</td><td>sink below the horizon<br>沉到地平线下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old boat began to sink soon after the storm.  暴风雨过后不久那条旧船开始下沉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tumble</td><td>/ˈtʌmbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>混乱，杂乱一堆</td><td>a tumble of clothes<br>一堆乱衣服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room was a tumble of boxes, books and old toys.  屋里是一堆乱七八糟的箱子、书和旧玩具。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 skyrocket</td><td>/ˈskaɪˌrɑːkət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>飞涨，猛升</td><td>prices skyrocket overnight<br>价格一夜飞涨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rents skyrocketed after the new factory opened downtown.  新工厂在市中心开业后房租飞涨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soar</td><td>/ˈsɔːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>猛增，飞升</td><td>costs soar<br>成本猛增</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Building costs soared during the second year of construction.  施工第二年建筑成本猛增。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>plump</b></td><td>/plʌmp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>丰满的，圆胖的；选定，支持</td><td>plump for the plan<br>选定支持这项计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of the villagers plumped for the cheaper of the two plans.  大多数村民选了两个方案中较便宜的那个。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chubby</td><td>/ˈtʃʌbi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>圆胖的，丰满的</td><td>chubby cheeks<br>圆胖的脸颊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The baby's chubby cheeks made everyone at the table smile.  婴儿圆胖的脸颊让桌边每个人都笑了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fleshy</td><td>/ˈfleʃi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多肉的，肥胖的</td><td>fleshy arms<br>多肉的手臂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He had a broad, fleshy face and kind eyes.  他有一张宽阔多肉的脸和一双慈祥的眼睛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fat</td><td>/ˈfæt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>肥胖的，多脂的</td><td>a fat tabby cat<br>一只肥胖的花斑猫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fat cat refused to move off the warm step.  那只肥猫拒绝离开温暖的台阶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gross</td><td>/ˈɡroʊs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过于肥胖的；粗俗的</td><td>a gross, bloated figure<br>臃肿肥胖的身形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The caricature gave the mayor a gross, swollen belly.  那幅漫画给市长画了个臃肿肥大的肚子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obese</td><td>/oʊˈbiːs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肥胖的，过度肥胖的</td><td>clinically obese<br>临床上属肥胖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic treats children who are clinically obese.  这家诊所治疗临床肥胖的儿童。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rotund</td><td>/roʊˈtʌnd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>圆胖的；圆浑的</td><td>a rotund gentleman<br>一位圆胖的先生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A rotund gentleman in a gray coat blocked the doorway.  一位穿灰外套的圆胖先生堵在门口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 round</td><td>/ˈraʊnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>圆的，圆形的</td><td>a small round window<br>小圆窗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They sat at a round table so everyone could see each other.  他们围着圆桌坐，这样人人都能看见对方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 advocate</td><td>/ˈædvəkeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，提倡</td><td>advocate reform<br>提倡改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many doctors advocate walking as the simplest form of exercise.  许多医生提倡把走路作为最简单的运动方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 back</td><td>/ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，资助</td><td>back a candidate<br>支持某位候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several investors agreed to back her new company.  几位投资人同意资助她的新公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 champion</td><td>/ˈtʃæmpiən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持</td><td>champion a cause<br>支持一项事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has championed the rights of migrant workers for years.  她多年来一直为农民工的权益奔走呼吁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endorse</td><td>/ɪnˈdɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开支持</td><td>endorse a candidate<br>公开支持某候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union voted to endorse the mayor for a second term.  工会投票支持市长连任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 patronize</td><td>/ˈpeɪtrənaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞助；以高人一等的态度对待</td><td>patronize the arts<br>赞助艺术</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wealthy merchants patronized young painters throughout the Renaissance.  富商在整个文艺复兴时期都赞助年轻画家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lean</td><td>/ˈliːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>瘦的，精瘦的</td><td>lean and muscular<br>精瘦而结实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of running left him lean and quick.  多年跑步让他精瘦而敏捷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slender</td><td>/ˈslendər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>苗条的，细长的</td><td>a slender waist<br>纤细的腰身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She held the flower by its slender stem.  她捏着那朵花细长的茎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 svelte</td><td>/svelt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（女人）体态苗条的，优雅的</td><td>a svelte silhouette<br>苗条优雅的身影</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her svelte silhouette drew admiring glances across the crowded ballroom.  她苗条优雅的身影在拥挤的舞厅里引来赞赏的目光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 thin</td><td>/ˈθɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>使变稀；薄的，瘦的</td><td>thin the paint slightly<br>把颜料稍微稀释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thin the sauce with a little warm water before serving.  上菜前用少量温水把酱汁调稀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 baffle</td><td>/ˈbæfl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使疑惑</td><td>baffle the experts<br>使专家困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strange symptoms continued to baffle the visiting experts.  这些奇怪的症状一直使来访的专家疑惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foil</td><td>/fɔɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>挫败</td><td>foil a plot<br>挫败阴谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Alert guards foiled the plot before anyone was hurt.  警觉的警卫在有人受伤之前挫败了阴谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frustrate</td><td>/ˈfrʌstreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻挠，使受挫；使沮丧</td><td>frustrate one's plans<br>使计划受挫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow frustrated our plans to drive home.  大雪打乱了我们开车回家的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sabotage</td><td>/ˈsæbətɑːʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>妨害，破坏</td><td>an act of sabotage<br>一次破坏行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investigators concluded that the fire was an act of deliberate sabotage.  调查人员认定这场火灾是一次故意的破坏行动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
